*Ningxin Pan,Hanyu Li,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出COMPLEXITYWORLD基准，包含390个任务、39个领域视觉世界和29个决策类别，用于评估视觉语言模型在需要全局约束满足的视觉决策任务上的表现。结果显示除GPT-5.6-Sol外所有模型接受率低于40%，而GPT-5.6-Sol达到75.6%，揭示了视觉到决策的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型评估主要关注图像内容的识别能力，但许多真实世界任务需要模型基于视觉证据做出满足全局约束的完整决策。现有基准缺乏对此类复杂视觉决策能力的系统评估。

Method: 构建COMPLEXITYWORLD基准：从隐藏的结构化规范生成390个任务，渲染为视觉场景，并使用可执行验证器对任何可行解决方案进行评分。任务覆盖39个领域视觉世界和29个决策类别。对比直接推理、结构化信息表示和代理脚手架等不同条件下的模型性能。

Result: 直接推理下，除GPT-5.6-Sol（75.6%接受率）外所有模型均低于40%验证器接受率；将决策信息显式结构化为文本时性能显著提升，但等价视觉呈现方式下性能差异很大；代理脚手架带来的提升较小且依赖于模型。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型存在持久的"视觉到决策"瓶颈，单纯的推理增强无法消除这一瓶颈。模型在视觉条件与结构化条件下表现差距明显，说明视觉信息到决策约束的转换是当前VLM的薄弱环节。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made rapid progress in visual perception and increasingly support real-world tasks that depend on images. Many such tasks, however, require more than rec- ognizing what an image contains: a model must use visual evidence to make a complete decision whose parts jointly satisfy global constraints. We introduce COMPLEXITYWORLD, a benchmark of 390 tasks across 39 domain-inspired visual worlds and 29 decision categories. Each task is generated from a hidden structured specification, rendered as a visual scene, and scored by an exe- cutable verifier that accepts any feasible solution. Under direct inference, all evaluated models ex- cept GPT-5.6-Sol remain below 40% verifier ac- ceptance rate (VAR), while GPT-5.6-Sol reaches 75.6%. Performance improves substantially when the same decision information is made explicit in structured form, yet varies sharply across equiva- lent visual presentations. Agent scaffolds provide smaller, model-dependent gains. Together, these results reveal a persistent visual-to-decision bot- tleneck that additional inference alone does not remove.

</details>


### [25] [LAVE: Latent Visual Evidence-Enhanced Planning for Video Tool-use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07585)
*Zijian Wang,Junnan Zhu,Rongzhen Li,Xiao Liu,Guohui Xiang,Quan Lu,Lijia Liu,Yining Wang,Jiang Zhong,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LAVE框架，通过双通道观察接口（可见文本通道+潜在视觉通道）复用已完成工具调用的潜在视觉证据，在不进行额外训练、帧回放或修改原始编排的情况下，解决视频工具使用代理中的"工具观察瓶颈"问题，在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和CG-Bench上均取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解中，视频工具使用代理通过文本观察进行工具-规划器通信，但文本接口对工具计算结果是有损的，导致未言语化的视觉证据被丢弃，无法用于后续规划。这一"工具观察瓶颈"限制了多步视频代理的规划能力。

Method: LAVE提出双通道观察接口：可见通道保留原始文本轨迹，潜在通道存储带工具角色、源帧时间戳和视觉位置的预言语视觉更新。规划时检索与当前规划器状态相关但未被文本覆盖的证据，通过有界时间戳对齐的潜在更新和熵约束帧时间路由进行集成。该框架无需训练，无需帧回放，也无需修改原始编排。

Result: 在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和CG-Bench上，LAVE在多个骨干网络上持续提升视频工具使用代理的性能。在可比帧预算下，LAVE在Video-MME总评分上比最强基线高出3.76分。

Conclusion: 潜在视觉证据复用对多步视频代理规划是有效的，证明未言语化的视觉信息可以在不增加训练开销的情况下显著提升长视频理解任务的规划性能。

Abstract: Long-video understanding requires models to efficiently acquire and reuse sparse visual evidence from long and redundant video streams. Recent video tool-use agents address this challenge by iteratively invoking visual Tools at different temporal scales, but their Tool-Planner communication typically relies on textual observations. Such text-only interfaces provide lossy summaries of Tool computations, causing previously computed visual evidence not verbalized to be discarded and unavailable for subsequent planning. We identify this limitation as the Tool observation bottleneck and propose Latent Visual Evidence-Enhanced Planning (LAVE), a training-free framework for reusing latent visual evidence from completed Tool calls. LAVE introduces a dual-channel observation interface: the visible channel preserves the original textual trajectory, while the latent channel stores pre-verbal visual updates with their Tool roles, source-frame timestamps, and visual locations. During planning, LAVE retrieves evidence relevant to the current Planner state but not covered by textual observations, and integrates it through bounded timestamp-aligned latent updates with entropy-constrained frame-time routing. This enables video agents to reuse existing visual computation without additional training, frame replay, or modifications to the original orchestration. Extensive experiments on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and CG-Bench show that LAVE consistently improves video tool-use agents across backbones. Under a comparable frame budget, LAVE improves the Video-MME overall score by 3.76 points over the strongest baseline, demonstrating the effectiveness of latent visual evidence reuse for multi-step video-agent planning.

</details>


### [26] [MAGIC-SSCIL: Manifold Anchoring and Geometric Incremental Calibration for Semi-Supervised Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07586)
*Yousef Abdi,Mohammad Asadpour,Yousef Seyfari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGIC框架，用于解决无样本存储的半监督类别增量学习问题。通过软权重几何校准（SWGC）和几何结构对齐（GSA）两个核心组件，在不存储旧数据的情况下稳定特征空间，防止灾难性遗忘。在CIFAR-100、CUB-200和ImageNet-R上超越多种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督类别增量学习在无样本存储（exemplar-free）设置下极具挑战性，现有方法因特征漂移而灾难性遗忘，且随着标签空间增长伪标签可靠性急剧下降。作者旨在解决这一核心难题。

Method: MAGIC框架包含两个核心组件：（1）软权重几何校准（SWGC），利用基于图的标签传播在可学习特征空间上加权校准冻结主干上的类均值和方差，从校准后的高斯分布中采样幻影特征替代旧任务数据；（2）几何结构对齐（GSA）目标，通过匹配学生和教师头的相对结构以及将特征锚点与固定分类器原型对齐，锁定特征空间方向，防止适配器漂移。

Result: 使用冻结的ResNet-18骨干和可学习的塑性适配器，在CIFAR-100、CUB-200和ImageNet-R数据集上，以1%、5%和10%的标签比率进行实验。MAGIC在平均增量精度上超越了大多数配备FixMatch的监督式CIL方法和原生SSCIL基线；最大的提升出现在细粒度、低标签设置下。

Conclusion: MAGIC通过几何校准和结构对齐有效解决了无样本SSCIL中的特征漂移问题，在不存储旧样本的前提下保持特征空间的稳定性，特别是在细粒度低标签场景下表现突出，这恰恰是置信度阈值方法最失效的场景。

Abstract: Semi-supervised Class Incremental Learning (SSCIL) is a severe challenge for neural networks, and it is hardest in the exemplar-free setting where no past data may be stored. Existing methods forget catastrophically due to feature drift, and their pseudo-labels become increasingly unreliable as the label space grows. In this paper, we propose MAGIC (Manifold Anchoring and Geometric Incremental Calibration), a framework that stabilizes plasticity without storing exemplars. MAGIC's design centers on two components. The first is Soft-Weighted Geometry Calibration (SWGC), which uses graph-based label propagation on the learner's plastic feature space to weight and calibrate class means and variances computed on the frozen backbone; from these calibrated Gaussians, we sample phantom features that stand in for data from previous tasks. The second is a Geometric Structural Alignment (GSA) objective that preserves representation topology by matching the relational structure of student and teacher heads and aligning feature anchors with the fixed classifier prototypes, locking the orientation of the feature space. Together, these constraints keep the adapter from drifting, so geometric relations between classes remain stable as new classes arrive. We implement MAGIC with a frozen ResNet-18 backbone and a learnable plastic adapter. Across CIFAR-100, CUB-200, and ImageNet-R, at label ratios of 1%, 5%, and 10%, MAGIC improves average incremental accuracy over most of the supervised CIL methods equipped with FixMatch and native SSCIL baselines; the largest gains occur in the fine-grained, low-label setting, where confidence thresholding fails most clearly.

</details>


### [27] [NewtonGS: Physics-Structured Object-Level Neural Newtonian Dynamics for Gaussian Scene Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07598)
*Lianlei Shan,Feiyang Ye,Yan Chen,Yong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NewtonGS是一个物理结构化的3D高斯场景动画框架，通过22维物体状态表示和Gaussian-NND动力学模型，实现物体级状态推演与高斯场景动画生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯方法主要重建时变场景或模拟形变，缺乏紧凑的物体级状态表示，无法实现直接可控的物体运动。

Method: 提出22维物体状态（包含位姿、线/角速度、各向异性尺度及其变化率、质量、接触属性），并设计Gaussian-NND模型，结合解析平移、四元数运动学、重力、阻尼和尺度恢复动力学与学习到的连续及接触残差；通过离散事件图处理地面接触，利用预测的位姿和尺度定义共享仿射变换更新所有关联高斯的均值和协方差。

Result: 在State-32数据集（含分布内和速度范围偏移两个子集）上，NewtonGS在轨迹RMSE、最终位移误差和速度RMSE上均优于五个解析基线；在Gaussian-32数据集上展示了从预测状态到高斯物体动画的有效转换。

Conclusion: NewtonGS填补了动态高斯方法中物体级状态控制的空白，为3D高斯场景提供了一种可控、紧凑且物理一致的物体动画框架。

Abstract: Animating objects in a static 3D Gaussian scene requires an explicit object-level dynamic state and a controllable model of object motion. Existing dynamic Gaussian methods primarily reconstruct time-varying scenes or simulate deformation, rather than provide compact object states for direct control. To address this gap, we present NewtonGS, a physics-structured framework for object-level state rollout and Gaussian scene animation. NewtonGS represents each object with a 22-dimensional state covering pose, linear and angular velocity, anisotropic scale and its rate, mass, and contact properties. Its Gaussian Neural Newtonian Dynamics (Gaussian-NND) model combines analytic translation, quaternion kinematics, gravity, damping, and scale-restoration dynamics with learned continuous and contact residuals. A discrete event map handles floor contact. Predicted poses and scales define a shared affine transformation that updates the means and covariances of all Gaussians associated with each object. We construct two procedurally generated datasets: State-32 for state-rollout evaluation and Gaussian-32 for state-to-Gaussian transformation. On both the in-distribution and velocity-range-shift splits of State-32, NewtonGS achieves lower trajectory RMSE, final displacement error, and velocity RMSE than five analytic baselines. Experiments on Gaussian-32 further demonstrate effective conversion from predicted states to animated Gaussian objects.

</details>


### [28] [HSMLA: Hierarchical Softmax Multi-scale Linear Attention for Efficient Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07616)
*Dong Liu,Yanxuan Yu,Renata Borovica-Gajic,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HSMLA（分层Softmax多尺度线性注意力），通过结合ReLU线性注意力、选择性Softmax细化和深度可分离卷积实现多尺度令牌表示，在保持线性注意力高效率的同时恢复局部上下文建模能力，在多种密集预测任务中实现了高达4.2倍的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉Transformer在高分辨率密集预测任务中因自注意力的二次复杂度而面临巨大的计算开销；现有的线性注意力虽然提升了效率，但牺牲了局部上下文建模能力，导致精度下降。

Method: HSMLA采用三种机制的组合：(1)基于ReLU的线性注意力用于建模全局上下文；(2)选择性Softmax细化用于处理关键局部特征；(3)通过深度可分离卷积获得多尺度令牌表示，从而兼顾全局与局部信息。

Result: 在密集预测任务中实现了最高4.2倍推理加速；在CT器官分割任务中达到87.3%的Dice分数并提升3.2倍速度；在病理WSI任务中达到94.2%的AUC并提升4.1倍速度。

Conclusion: HSMLA在精度与效率之间取得了优越的平衡，证明了通过多层次注意力机制设计可以有效解决线性注意力缺乏局部建模的问题，同时保持显著的推理加速优势。

Abstract: Vision transformers face significant computational overheads in high-resolution dense prediction due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Linear attention offers efficiency but sacrifices local context modeling. We propose \textbf{HSMLA (Hierarchical Softmax Multi-scale Linear Attention)}, which combines ReLU-based linear attention for global context, selective softmax refinement for critical local features, and multi-scale token representations via depthwise convolutions. HSMLA achieves superior accuracy-efficiency trade-offs: up to $4.2\times$ inference-time speedup across dense prediction tasks, $87.3%$ Dice with $3.2\times$ speedup on CT organ segmentation, and $94.2%$ AUC with $4.1\times$ speedup on pathology WSI.

</details>


### [29] [FlowErase-OPD: Multi-Concept Erasure via Anchored On-Policy Distillation in Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07620)
*Yi Sun,Yimin Zhou,Xinhao Zhong,Zhiqi Zhang,Junhao Li,Bin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowErase-OPD是一个基于在线策略蒸馏(OPD)的多概念擦除框架，通过将多个单概念擦除模型蒸馏为统一的LoRA模块，并引入锚定多教师蒸馏和自适应保留控制，在保持生成能力的同时高效擦除多个不安全概念，达到最先进性能并具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配模型概念擦除方法主要聚焦于单个概念的移除，而同时有效擦除多个概念仍然具有挑战性，缺乏一个统一且协调的多概念擦除框架。

Method: 首先将多个单概念擦除模型蒸馏到统一的LoRA模块中；引入锚定多教师蒸馏(AMTD)，包含保留教师以缓解概念擦除与生成能力保留之间的权衡；提出自适应保留控制(ARC)，动态调整各擦除教师的采样频率、损失权重以及擦除与保留教师的相对贡献。

Result: 在裸体、物体和艺术风格擦除的广泛实验中，FlowErase-OPD在擦除效果、图像质量和语义对齐之间取得了更好的权衡，在多种多概念擦除场景中达到最先进性能，且对对抗攻击具有很强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在线策略蒸馏可作为流匹配模型中安全可控生成的原则性框架，FlowErase-OPD验证了其在多概念擦除任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in flow matching models have substantially improved the quality of text-to-image generation, but have also raised increasing safety concerns due to their potential to generate harmful or undesirable content. Existing concept erasure methods for flow matching models predominantly focus on removing individual concepts, while effectively erasing multiple concepts simultaneously remains challenging. We propose FlowErase-OPD, a framework for multi-concept erasure based on on-policy distillation (OPD). Our approach first distills multiple single-concept erased models into a unified LoRA module and introduces Anchored Multi-Teacher Distillation (AMTD), which incorporates a retention teacher to mitigate the trade-off between concept erasure and preservation of generative capabilities. To further improve the coordination of multiple erasure objectives, we develop Adaptive Retention Control (ARC), which dynamically adjusts the sampling frequency and loss weight of each erasure teacher, together with the relative contribution of erasure and retention teachers throughout training. Extensive experiments on nudity, object, and artistic-style erasure demonstrate that FlowErase-OPD consistently improves the trade-off between erasure effectiveness, image quality, and semantic alignment, achieving state-of-the-art performance across diverse multi-concept erasure settings. Furthermore, the resulting models exhibit strong robustness against adversarial attacks. These results highlight the potential of on-policy distillation as a principled framework for safe and controllable generation in flow matching models.

</details>


### [30] [HeatCast: A Benchmark for Neighborhood-Scale LST Forecasting across 124 U.S. Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07640)
*Jesus Guerrero,Isaac Corley,Leon Najafirad,Maryam Tabar,Paul Rad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeatCast是一个基于Landsat的月度LST（地表温度）预测基准，覆盖美国124个城市、2013年至2025年6月，提供30米分辨率的多通道数据（含LST、高程、RGB、光谱指数、反照率、质量掩膜和局部气候区标签）。论文评估了CNN+LSTM和Earthformer两种模型，其中Earthformer在次月预测中取得7.74 K RMSE，优于CNN+LSTM的10.42 K；仅用八个非LST通道预测可达7.72 K，优于单独使用LST历史（8.15 K）或RGB（8.68 K）。数据、代码和权重已以MIT许可证公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LST预测研究通常仅覆盖一到三个城市、使用千米级产品，且大多不公开数据与代码，缺乏一个共享的30米分辨率LST预测基准，阻碍了方法可复现性和公平比较。

Method: 构建HeatCast基准，包含124个美国城市30米月度LST瓦片及多源辅助通道，设定固定时间划分、LCZ分层评估指标和参考评估框架；评估CNN+LSTM和Earthformer两种深度学习模型进行次月预测，并进行多通道消融实验。

Result: Earthformer达到7.74 K RMSE，优于CNN+LSTM的10.42 K；仅用非LST八通道预测达7.72 K，优于LST历史（8.15 K）和RGB（8.68 K）。数据与代码已公开。

Conclusion: HeatCast填补了LST预测领域缺乏大规模、高分辨率共享基准的空白，证明了多通道特征（尤其非LST通道）在LST预测中的有效性，并为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Land Surface Temperature (LST) is a widely used satellite-derived measure of urban surface heat, but there is no shared benchmark for forecasting it at 30 m. Prior studies usually cover one to three cities, use kilometer-scale products, or do not release data and code. We introduce HeatCast, a Landsat-based benchmark for monthly LST forecasting across 124 U.S. cities from 2013 through June 2025. HeatCast contains 30 m monthly tiles with LST, elevation, surfacereflectance RGB, three spectral indices, broadband albedo, quality masks, and Local Climate Zone (LCZ) labels, together with a fixed temporal split, LCZ-stratified metrics, and a reference evaluation harness. We evaluate a CNN+LSTM and Earthformer on next-month forecasting, where Earthformer reaches 7.74 K RMSE against 10.42 K for the CNN+LSTM. Forecasting from the eight nonLST channels alone reaches 7.72 K, against 8.15 K from LST history and 8.68 K from RGB. The data, code, and weights are released under MIT at https://doi.org/10.57967/hf/9889.

</details>


### [31] [Data collection from highways: a geometric, class-agnostic approach to embedded vehicle counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07643)
*Lucas Gouveia Omena Lopes,William W. M. Lira,Alexandre M. Lima,Thales M. A. Vieira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种纯几何的交通流量检测管线，在树莓派级硬件上无需训练目标检测器即可实时统计车辆。通过背景差分与阈值分割检测运动物体，并用虚拟感应线圈的几何规则进行计数。分析了两种计数规则（恒定平均速度规则和自校准预标定规则），后者在四段视频中达到83.3%-100%的准确率，现场部署达到91%，远超同算力下的基线（37.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前交通数据采集依赖深度目标检测器加跟踪，但这一流程预设了检测器已针对目标类别训练好——这在实际中往往不成立。作者希望探索一种无需训练数据的纯几何交通传感方案，适用于单板计算机等算力受限的边缘设备。

Method: 采用类无关的检测范式：用背景差分和阈值分割检测运动物体，无需目标模型、训练集或逐目标轨迹。设计两种计数规则：(1)恒定平均速度规则，基于高斯速度分布推导出约86%的理论准确率；(2)自校准预标定规则，从blob统计中恢复车道几何并统计车道占用边缘，同时免费获得平均车速。在Python和C++两种实现中比较性能和热可行性。

Result: 自校准预标定规则在四段视频中达到83.3%-100%准确率；现场部署中达91%，而相同算力下基于blob跟踪的基线仅为37.5%。此外还报告了关键发现：分辨率阈值、帧率下限、短频剪辑与长期未受控素材的差距，以及Python(100% CPU)与C++(40% CPU)的权衡。

Conclusion: 基于运动检测的类无关计数方案在开放类别、无标注数据、严格功耗限制、隐私受限场景以及冷启动引导学习型检测器等方面仍是正确选择。这些约束源于采样几何本身，而非当时特定的硬件限制，对当今边缘部署仍具指导意义。

Abstract: Traffic data collection is dominated today by deep object detectors followed by tracking-by-detection, a pipeline that presupposes what is often missing in practice: a detector already trained on the class one wants to count. We revisit a purely geometric traffic-sensing pipeline for Single Board Computers in which detection is class-agnostic: moving objects come from background subtraction and thresholding, and counting is decided by a geometric rule on an imaginary line across the road, a software inductive loop detector. With no object model, training set or per-object trajectory, it runs faster than real time on Raspberry Pi class hardware. Two counting rules are described: a constant average speed rule, whose expected accuracy is derived analytically as about 86% under a Gaussian speed distribution, and a self-calibrating pre-calibration rule that recovers the lane geometry from blob statistics and counts edges of lane occupancy, additionally yielding per-vehicle average speed at no extra cost. Over four videos the latter counts with 83.3%-100% accuracy; in a field deployment it reaches 91% against 37.5% for a blob-tracking baseline under the same compute budget. We report the observations of that period in detail: the resolution floor below which accuracy collapses, the frame rate floor at which vehicles alias past the counting line, the gap between short curated clips and long uncontrolled footage, and the trade-off between Python (easier to tune, 100% CPU) and C++ (40% CPU, thermally viable). These are properties of the sampling geometry, not of the hardware of the time, and still constrain edge deployments. We close by arguing where motion-based, class-agnostic detection remains the right tool: open-set classes with no annotated data, tight power budgets, privacy-constrained installations, and the cold start of mining training crops to bootstrap a learned detector.

</details>


### [32] [Keep It Simple: Multi-Key Episodic Memory Retrieval for Ultra-Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07663)
*Yeeun Choi,Youngbeom Yoo,Joon-Young Lee,Hyolim Kang,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MERIT是一个针对超长视频理解的高效智能体框架，采用多键情节检索+推理时时间扩展的策略，通过简单的键匹配机制实现高召回率记忆检索，并在推理阶段按需扩展时间范围，在三个长视频基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长视频（数小时到数天）无法被现有MLLM端到端处理，需要两阶段范式。以往工作投入复杂的内存构建来预建模视频中的高层关系，但构建时并不知道下游查询，导致成本高且不灵活。作者主张在内存构建阶段优先保证高召回率的可检索性，将查询特定的高层关系组合推迟到推理阶段。

Method: MERIT框架包含两个核心组件：1）情节式多键表示（Episodic Multi-key Representation），通过简单的键匹配机制实现细粒度记忆的精确检索；2）邻居过滤机制（Neighbor Filtering），仅在检索到的片段周围按需扩展时间范围来捕获更广泛的语义上下文，避免全局内存构建的巨大计算开销。

Result: MERIT在三个长视频基准（EgoLifeQA、LVBench和Video-MME Long）上均取得了最先进的性能表现，证明了简单键匹配+按需时间扩展策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在内存构建阶段简化模型（只追求高召回率检索），并将复杂的高层关系组合推迟到推理阶段，是一种比预先构建复杂视频关系更有效且计算更高效的超长视频理解方案。

Abstract: When videos extend from hours to days, directly processing them end-to-end becomes impractical for current Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs). This ultra-long setting necessitates a two-stage paradigm: query-agnostic memory construction followed by retrieval-based inference. Prior work invests in complex memory construction to pre-model high-level relations in videos, despite not knowing the downstream query at build time. We instead prioritize high-recall retrievability during memory building, and defer query-specific, high-level relation composition to inference time. To this end, we propose MERIT(Multi-key Episodic Retrieval with Inference-time Temporal expansion), a simple yet effective agentic framework for ultra-long video understanding. First, we formulate an episodic multi-key representation that enables precise retrieval of fine-grained memories through a simple key-matching mechanism. Second, we introduce a neighbor filtering mechanism to capture broader semantic context without the massive computational overhead of global memory construction. This is achieved by expanding the temporal scope exclusively around the retrieved segments at inference time. By leveraging simple key-matching with this on-demand temporal expansion, MERIT achieves state-of-the-art performance across three long-video benchmarks: EgoLifeQA, LVBench, and Video-MME (Long).

</details>


### [33] [CosmosAlign: Adapting a World Foundation Model for Generative Traffic Video Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07693)
*Quang Minh Dinh,Tuan Kiet Doan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CosmosAlign是一个基于预训练Cosmos3-Nano世界基础模型的生成式交通视频预测框架,采用两阶段LoRA适配策略(先对齐条件分布,再通过LLM重写字幕对齐提示接口),并在推理时使用基于共识的中位数样本选择和运动自适应静态区域混合的无训练优化方法,在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 5基准上以76.49分排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到,将大型预训练世界模型适配到下游预测任务时,关键在于分布对齐而非增加模型容量,因此需要一个有效的适配策略来释放预训练模型的潜力。

Method: 提出两阶段LoRA适配策略:(1) 第一阶段对齐条件分布与目标预测任务;(2) 第二阶段通过LLM重写流水线将训练字幕与模型原生结构化提示接口对齐。推理阶段采用无训练的共识中位数样本选择与运动自适应静态区域混合来改善预测质量。

Result: 在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 5基准上取得76.49分,在最终排行榜上排名第一。

Conclusion: 证明了分布对齐是适配预训练世界模型到下游预测任务的关键,而非扩大模型容量;两阶段LoRA策略配合无训练推理优化能有效提升生成式交通视频预测性能。

Abstract: Generative traffic video forecasting aims to synthesize long-horizon, temporally coherent future videos of traffic scenes from a short observation history and textual descriptions. In this paper, we present CosmosAlign, a generative traffic video forecasting framework built upon the pretrained Cosmos3-Nano world foundation model. Our approach is motivated by the observation that successfully adapting large pretrained world models to downstream forecasting tasks depends primarily on distribution alignment rather than increased model capacity. To this end, we propose a two-stage LoRA adaptation strategy that first aligns the conditioning-mode distribution with the target forecasting task, and then aligns the training captions with the model's native structured prompting interface through an LLM-based re-captioning pipeline. During inference, we further improve prediction quality using a fully training-free procedure consisting of consensus-based medoid sample selection and motion-adaptive blending of static scene regions. CosmosAlign achieves a final score of 76.49 on the AI City Challenge 2026 Track 5 benchmark, ranking first on the final leaderboard. Our code is publicly available at https://quangminhdinh.github.io/CosmosAlign/.

</details>


### [34] [SpikeWorld: Fast-State Adaptation for Frozen Spiking World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07712)
*Ziqiao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpikeWorld，一个145万参数的稀疏脉冲模型，联合训练异构感知预测、语义、图像-文本绑定和动作条件动力学。部署时所有参数冻结，通过延迟下一状态残差更新两个外部路径（累计固定库损失选择有界动作修正、路由特定残差矩阵细化下一状态预测）。无需标签、奖励或真实移位值。联合优化使动作下一状态MSE提升17.10%，同时改善多模态预测、语义准确率和图像-文本检索。在保留剪切和衰减流上，聚合预测提升5.48%和30.01%；固定库动作路径跟踪提升24.20%和3.94%。Meta-World六臂研究（450条轨迹）中，冻结策略奖励提升7.90，成功差13.33点。模型参数与语义输出在相同输入下逐位不变。16字节RLS估计器在线性衰减上获得最高非预言奖励，表明贡献非在优异线性识别，而在与冻结多模态脉冲检查点的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测模型在部署后难以利用自监督信号：当动力学和语义共享参数时，冻结参数阻止自适应，而权重更新需要优化器状态且可能改变已学表征。现有方法无法在保持语义不变的前提下实现部署期自适应。

Method: 提出SpikeWorld稀疏脉冲模型联合训练感知预测、语义、图像-文本绑定和动作条件动力学。部署时所有参数冻结，通过两个外部路径实现自适应：累计固定库损失选择有界动作修正，路由特定残差矩阵精化下一状态预测。两条路径均不使用标签、教师输出、奖励、成功信号或真实移位值。

Result: 联合优化使动作下一状态MSE提升17.10%，同时改善多模态预测、语义准确率和图像-文本检索。保留剪切/衰减流上聚合预测提升5.48%/30.01%，动作路径跟踪提升24.20%/3.94%。Meta-World六臂研究中冻结策略奖励提升7.90（95% CI [2.48, 14.06]），成功差13.33点。16字节RLS估计器在线性衰减上获得最高非预言奖励。

Conclusion: SpikeWorld证明了在不更新任何训练参数的前提下，通过外部路径整合自监督信号可实现有效的部署期自适应。核心贡献并非线性识别能力，而是与冻结的多模态脉冲检查点的集成方式，且不改变模型语义输出。代码已开源。

Abstract: A predictive model receives a self-supervised signal whenever the consequence of an action is observed. Using that signal after deployment is difficult when dynamics and semantics share parameters: freezing prevents adaptation, whereas weight updates require optimizer state and may alter the learned representation. Here we introduce SpikeWorld, a 1.45M-parameter sparse spiking model jointly trained for heterogeneous sensory prediction, semantics, image-text binding and action-conditioned dynamics. At deployment, all trained parameters are frozen. Delayed next-state residuals update two external paths: cumulative fixed-bank losses select the bounded action correction, while route-specific residual matrices refine next-state prediction. Neither path uses labels, teacher outputs, rewards, success signals or the true shift value. Joint optimization improves action next-state MSE by 17.10\% while also improving multimodal prediction, semantic accuracy and image-text retrieval. On held-out shear and attenuation streams, the combined external state improves aggregate prediction by 5.48\% and 30.01\%; its fixed-bank action path improves tracking by 24.20\% and 3.94\%, respectively. In a six-arm study comprising 450 new Meta-World trajectories (75 per arm), SpikeWorld raises frozen-policy reward by 7.90 (95\% CI [2.48, 14.06]); the 13.33-point success difference is descriptive (CI [0, 40]). For identical sensory inputs, model parameters and inherited semantic outputs remain bitwise unchanged. A 16-byte RLS estimator obtains the highest non-oracle reward on linear attenuation, showing that the contribution is not superior linear identification, but its integration with a frozen multimodal spiking checkpoint. Reference code is publicly available at https://github.com/Oooorca/SpikeWorld.

</details>


### [35] [Tokenizer Generator Coupling in Medical Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07713)
*Liam Chalcroft*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在受控的ChestMNIST 64x64设置下，系统研究了潜在医学图像生成中tokenizer、生成器和采样器三者之间的耦合关系。发现三者排名依赖相互作用，重建PSNR不可靠，提出生成器无关的统计量（neighbour-conditional predictive gain）可有效分离量化器家族，并在LFQ-1024上通过重调采样器将FID从0.44/0.41降至0.09/0.10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在医学图像生成器通常将tokenizer视为固定的预处理步骤，但这种分离假设是否有效缺乏验证。作者希望在受控条件下交叉检验离散tokenizer、生成器家族和采样器设置之间的相互作用，以确定tokenizer与生成器是否真正可以独立优化。

Method: 在ChestMNIST 64x64上，构建共享潜在网格，交叉离散tokenizer、生成器家族和采样器设置，并设置连续潜在参考单元。使用三个种子重训词汇量1024的交互模块验证交互稳定性（9对量化器比较中6对超过三个种子标准差）。提出生成器无关统计量——neighbour-conditional predictive gain用于分离量化器家族。在LFQ-1024上对D3PM和SE-D3PM在验证集上重新调优采样器参数，并用FID-192作为内部排名指标（与FID-2048 Spearman相关0.80）。

Result: 排名依赖tokenizer、生成器和采样器的联合作用：最佳量化器随生成器变化，验证集采样器选择改变生成器排名。重建PSNR不可靠，而neighbour-conditional predictive gain以rank-AUC 1.00准确分离量化器家族。LFQ-1024上重调采样器使D3PM/SE-D3PM的FID-192从0.44/0.41降至0.09/0.10且跨种子可复现。FID-192与FID-2048（Spearman 0.80）及无标签分类器双样本检验（0.78）一致排名。

Conclusion: tokenizer不能被视为固定预处理，模型能力（modelability）是条件于生成器、采样器和推理预算的。提出率失真-模型能力（rate-distortion-modelability）框架来解释结果。所有结论严格限定在64x64低分辨率医学风格图像、无条件生成和非临床FID指标范围内。

Abstract: Latent medical image generators usually treat the tokenizer as fixed preprocessing. We test whether this separation is valid in a controlled ChestMNIST study at 64x64, crossing discrete tokenizers, generator families, and sampler settings under a shared latent grid, with continuous-latent reference cells. In this controlled setting, rankings depend jointly on the tokenizer, generator, and sampler: the best quantizer changes with the generator, and validation-based sampler selection changes the apparent generator ranking. We retrain the vocabulary-1024 interaction block at three seeds and the interaction survives (6 of 9 pairwise quantizer comparisons exceed three seed standard deviations), and we scope the wider single-seed grid accordingly. Reconstruction PSNR alone is not a reliable selection criterion; we instead introduce a generator-free statistic, neighbour-conditional predictive gain, that separates the quantizer families by downstream generation quality (rank-AUC 1.00) where reconstruction PSNR and marginal token entropy do not. On LFQ-1024, retuning D3PM and SE-D3PM (selected on a held-out validation split) moves them from default FID-192 0.44/0.41 to 0.09/0.10 at lower NFE, replicated across seeds; the continuous references were not given an equivalent sampler sweep. We report FID-192 as an internal ranking metric; it ranks consistently with standard FID-2048 (Spearman 0.80) and with a label-free classifier two-sample test (0.78). We interpret these results through a rate-distortion-modelability framing, where modelability is conditional on the generator, sampler, and inference budget. All experiments are at 64x64 on low-resolution medical-style images, unconditional, and evaluated with non-clinical FID-based metrics, and we scope every claim to that setting. Code: https://github.com/liamchalcroft/medtokenizers and https://github.com/liamchalcroft/medlatents.

</details>


### [36] [Vision Meets WiFi: Physics-Grounded Estimation of Volumetric Mechanical Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07726)
*Ali Bahri,Hongliang Li,Soufiane Lamghari,Jie Chuai,Zhitang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViWi是一个结合视觉和WiFi射频信号的对象中心框架，通过材料槽（material slots）对体素进行分组聚合，利用RF描述符提供全局材料组成线索，从而解决仅凭视觉进行体积力学属性（杨氏模量、泊松比、密度）估计时的模糊性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉单独估计每个体素的体积力学属性存在固有的模糊性，因为外观相似的对象可能具有不同的材料组成和物理行为。现有方法独立预测每个体素的属性，忽略了真实对象的分段常数材料结构，导致同一材料体素间的估计不一致，且缺乏解决视觉模糊性的显式机制。

Method: ViWi采用对象中心框架，使用紧凑的材料槽集合来表示对象，这些槽聚合共享材料身份的体素证据并产生一致的槽级属性预测。同时引入通过WiFi频段电磁模拟生成的紧凑RF描述符（基于介电常数和电导率），该描述符为材料槽提供全局组成线索以补充视觉信息，而视觉特征则保留体素级的空间定位能力。

Result: 在体积力学属性和质量估计基准上，ViWi在六个逐体素指标中的四个上超越了现有最先进方法；其纯视觉变体在所有质量估计指标上均有提升。

Conclusion: 将对象中心的材料结构与互补的RF证据相结合，能够实现比仅依赖视觉外观更准确且物理一致的体积属性估计。

Abstract: Estimating volumetric mechanical properties, including Young's modulus, Poisson's ratio, and density at each voxel, is intrinsically ambiguous from vision alone, as visually similar objects may have substantially different material compositions and physical behavior. Existing approaches predict these properties independently across voxels, overlooking the piecewise-constant material structure of real objects and producing noisy or inconsistent estimates for voxels that share the same material, while lacking an explicit mechanism to resolve visual ambiguity. We introduce ViWi (Vision Meets WiFi), an object-centric framework for volumetric mechanical-property estimation. ViWi represents each object using a compact set of material slots that aggregate evidence from voxels with a shared material identity and produce coherent slot-level property predictions. To complement visual appearance, ViWi incorporates a compact RF descriptor generated through WiFi-band electromagnetic simulation using permittivity and conductivity. The RF descriptor conditions the material slots with global composition cues that may be unavailable from images, while visual features preserve voxel-level spatial localization. Across volumetric mechanical-property and mass-estimation benchmarks, ViWi improves over the prior state of the art on four of six per-voxel metrics, while its vision-only variant improves all mass-estimation metrics. These results demonstrate that combining object-centric material structure with complementary RF evidence enables more accurate and physically coherent volumetric property estimation beyond what is possible from visual appearance alone.

</details>


### [37] [Ghost Features and Spooky Transfer Learning for Hypercomplex-Valued Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07735)
*Guilherme Vieira Neto,Marcos Eduardo Valle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过超复数神经网络层生成"幽灵特征"，利用额外虚部增强传统实数层的信息表达，并通过"幽灵迁移学习"将其融入已训练网络，从而构建更高效的神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超复数通过引入额外虚部提供代数和几何性质，在机器学习中具有潜力。作者旨在利用这些性质增强神经网络的表现力，同时保持与传统实数层输出的对应关系。

Method: 作者设计了超复数值的神经网络层，其中实部对应传统实数层输出，虚部则产生"幽灵特征"以包含增强信息。通过"幽灵迁移学习"过程，将幽灵特征有效集成到已训练的网络中。

Result: 幽灵特征包含实数层输出中不存在的信息，可显著提升神经网络效率，且源代码和Jupyter Notebook已公开供复现。

Conclusion: 超复数层产生的幽灵特征及相应的迁移学习方式为构建更高效的神经网络提供了新思路，展示了超复数在深度学习中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Hypercomplex numbers extend the concept of complex numbers by introducing additional imaginary components. Besides increasing dimensionality, operations on the imaginary parts provide algebraic and geometrical properties that can be beneficial for solving machine learning problems. In this paper, we show how to create hypercomplex-valued neural network layers where the real part corresponds to the output of a traditional real-valued layer. The additional imaginary parts of these hypercomplex-valued layers produce what we call ``ghost features,'' which contain enhanced information that is not present in the output of the real-valued layer. Moreover, ghost features can be effectively integrated into a trained neural network through a process we refer to as ``spooky transfer learning.'' This approach allows us to harness the richness of ghost features, leading to more efficient neural networks. The source code and Jupyter Notebook are available at https://github.com/mevalle/v-nets/.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在化学和数学推理任务的多数据集上对BRUCE进行了评估，实现了对VLM在视觉扰动下推理性能退化的量化分析，并通过四类推理领域及其细粒度子类型的划分，提供了可解释的故障分析结果。

Conclusion: BRUCE为多模态科学推理模型提供了系统化的鲁棒性基准框架，通过新指标和细粒度失败分析，揭示了VLM在不同视觉退化条件下的推理脆弱性模式，有助于指导更鲁棒模型的开发。

Abstract: Visual-language models (VLMs) frequently struggle with robustness issues in real-world situations due to low- or varying-quality input images. In this paper, we aim at analyzing VLMs' robustness by applying perturbations and distortions to the input images, such as blur or low contrast. Toward this goal, we propose BRUCE (Benchmarking Robustness Under Corruption Escalation, a multimodal reasoning fragility framework for scientific vision-language reasoning.
  State-of-the-art evaluation frameworks/studies primarily focus on clean-task accuracy and rarely analyze how reasoning stability degrades across robustness dimensions. Besides varying over a wide-range of input perturbations, BRUCE employs two novel metrics -- Robustness Corruption Index (RCI) and Traversal-RCI (T-RCI) -- to quantify how rapidly multimodal reasoning performance deteriorates in VLMs as visual corruption severity increases under progressive perturbation scaling. We evaluate BRUCE across chemistry and mathematical reasoning tasks for multiple datasets, while analyzing corruption-induced prediction failures in terms of four high-level reasoning domains: OCR-dependent reasoning, spatial reasoning, symbolic reasoning, and semantic failures, with each containing fine-grained corruption specific failure subtypes, thereby enabling an interpretable failure analysis.

</details>

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRSA框架，将参数高效微调分解为密集低秩成分和动态结构化稀疏低秩成分，分别捕捉全局共享任务结构和局部残差方向，在乳腺密度分类任务的外部域泛化上取得了优于最强对比方法的结果。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在DINOv3-Base上评估四类乳腺密度分类任务（VinDr-Mammo为源域，MammosighTR和RSNA为未见外部域）。LoRSA在内部验证集上保持竞争力，并在两个外部数据集上取得最佳macro-F1：比最强对比方法在MammosighTR上提升2.15个百分点，在RSNA上提升3.09个百分点。权重矩阵分析显示约92%的每个适应组件能量位于另一个组件的双侧奇异子空间之外，表明两个组件学到了高度互补的更新方向。

Conclusion: 将适配容量组织为独立的全局路径和残差路径可以改善参数高效适配的生物医学视觉模型在外部域上的泛化能力，验证了全局-残差分解策略的有效性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables the adaptation of vision foundation models to biomedical tasks under limited computational resources, but a single low-rank update can constrain all task-specific changes to one narrow parameter subspace. This restriction may prevent the model from simultaneously representing globally shared task structure and localized residual directions required for generalization to unseen imaging domains. We introduce LoRSA, a global--residual adaptation framework that jointly learns a dense low-rank component and a dynamically structured-sparse low-rank component. The dense component captures globally coordinated task adaptation, while the structured component provides complementary residual corrections whose support evolves during training. We characterize the representational capacity, approximation properties, rank structure, and singular-subspace complementarity of this decomposition. We evaluate LoRSA for four-class breast-density classification using DINOv3-Base, with VinDr-Mammo as the source domain and MammosighTR and RSNA as unseen external domains. LoRSA remains competitive on the internal validation set and achieves the best external macro-F1 on both target datasets, improving upon the strongest competing method by 2.15 percentage points on MammosighTR and 3.09 percentage points on RSNA. Weight-matrix analysis further shows that approximately $92\%$ of the energy of each adaptation component lies outside the bilateral singular subspace of the other, indicating that the two components learn largely complementary update directions. These results suggest that organizing adaptation capacity into distinct global and residual paths can improve the external-domain generalization of parameter-efficiently adapted biomedical vision models.

</details>

*Cong Chen,Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Jonathan Petit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用多任务感知间推理输出不一致性来检测对抗攻击的方案，在BDD100k验证集上对PGD攻击实现了99.9%的ROC-AUC检测性能。

Method: 利用复杂视觉系统中的多任务感知，通过多个视觉任务（如目标检测和实例分割）推理输出之间的不一致性来检测对抗扰动。具体包括：设计一致性评分指标来衡量任务间不一致性，并设计方法选择最佳模型对以有效检测不一致性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于多任务感知不一致性的对抗攻击检测方法既高效又有效，为资源受限环境下的自动驾驶视觉系统提供了实用的防御方案。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) have found successful deployment in numerous vision perception systems. However, their susceptibility to adversarial attacks has prompted concerns regarding their practical applications, specifically in the context of autonomous driving. Existing defenses often suffer from cost inefficiency, rendering their deployment impractical for resource-constrained applications. In this work, we propose an efficient and effective adversarial attack detection scheme leveraging the multi-task perception within a complex vision system. Adversarial perturbations are detected by the inconsistencies between the inference outputs of multiple vision tasks, e.g., object detection and instance segmentation. To this end, we developed a consistency score metric to measure the inconsistency between vision tasks. Next, we designed an approach to select the best model pairs for detecting inconsistencies effectively. Finally, we evaluated our defense against PGD attacks across multiple vision models on the BDD100k validation dataset. The experimental results demonstrated that our defense achieved a ROC-AUC performance of 99.9% detection within the considered attacker model.

</details>

*Seongyong Kim,Jingdao Chen,Yong Kwon Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了工地场景中单次LiDAR扫描的3D语义分割问题，提出了一种基于入射角感知的采样策略，该策略将点映射到几何归一化的流形空间进行体素选择，同时保留原始欧氏坐标用于下游学习。在SIP基准上，该方法在Point Transformer和PointNeXt上均提升了分割性能，尤其对非平面元素和梯子的分割效果更好，并降低了对采样分辨率的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景理解方法大多基于精心整理的数据集，无法反映真实工地扫描条件（覆盖不完整、密度不均、平面与稀疏构件严重失衡）。此外，大规模点云必须降采样，而采样策略直接影响几何细节与空间上下文的平衡，现有方法将采样视为通用预处理而非主动的分割组件。

Method: 提出一种入射感知的采样策略：在固定逐块点预算下，将点云映射到几何归一化的流形空间进行基于体素的选择，同时保留原始欧氏坐标供下游学习使用。该方法仅需要点坐标和法向量，无需修改骨干网络。

Result: 在Site in Pieces (SIP)基准上，使用Point Transformer和PointNeXt进行实验，结果显示该方法在分辨率平均分割性能上有所提升，尤其是对非平面元素和梯子的分割效果显著改善，同时降低了对采样分辨率的敏感性。

Conclusion: 采集感知的采样策略能够提供更稳定的几何表示，应视为单次扫描3D分割的主动组件而非通用预处理步骤。

Abstract: 3D scene understanding is increasingly important in construction, yet most methods are developed on curated datasets that do not fully reflect real site sensing conditions. In many workflows, individual LiDAR scans provide rapid local updates rather than complete scene representations, producing limited surface coverage, acquisition-driven density variation, and severe imbalance between dominant planar surfaces and sparse construction elements. Because large point clouds must be downsampled, sampling resolution and point allocation directly affect the balance between geometric detail and spatial context. This study evaluates these effects under a fixed per-fragment point budget and introduces an incidence-aware sampling strategy for individual LiDAR scans. The method maps points to a geometry-normalized manifold space for voxel-based selection while preserving original Euclidean coordinates for downstream learning. It requires only point coordinates and normals and no backbone modification. Using the Site in Pieces (SIP) benchmark, experiments with Point Transformer and PointNeXt show improved resolution-averaged segmentation performance, especially for non-planar elements and ladders, while reducing sensitivity to sampling resolution. The results show that acquisition-aware sampling can provide a more stable geometric representation and should be treated as an active component of individual-scan 3D segmentation rather than generic preprocessing.

</details>


### [42] [XClipGS: Exact Half-Space Clipping for Medical Volume Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07760)
*Zhongpai Gao,Benjamin Planche,Meng Zheng,Anwesa Choudhuri,Chaoyi Zhou,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出XClipGS，一种用于医学体积扫描的Gaussian-splatting精确裁剪方法。它将渲染时裁剪算子与内部监督视为两个独立问题，利用EWA splatting局部仿射模型将半空间限制高斯的光线积分分解为常规2D足迹与条件高斯CDF的闭式乘积，无需任何学习参数或辅助网络即可实现可微的逐像素裁剪。在八个CT和MRI体积上，XClipGS在PSNR、渲染速度及切面保真度等指标上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gaussian-splatting代理虽能实现医学体积扫描的交互式渲染，但裁剪平面会暴露不受外部视角训练约束的解剖结构，并切割图元，而传统splatting只能整体保留或丢弃图元。现有方法无法在渲染时精确处理裁剪与内部结构监督这两个问题。

Method: 1) 将问题分解为渲染时裁剪算子与内部监督两个独立部分；2) 在EWA splatting的局部仿射模型下，将半空间限制高斯的光线积分精确分解为普通2D足迹与条件高斯CDF的乘积，得到闭式逐像素算子（无学习参数、无辅助网络、对图元和平面对可微）；3) 使用多距离参考视图和变化裁剪平面轴与偏移来监督内部；4) 引入配对裁剪/未裁剪切面协议及两个新指标：差分参考裁剪误差（CDE）与剔除侧泄漏（Leak）。

Result: 在八个CT和MRI体积（使用训练中未见过的平面偏移量）上，XClipGS在每个体积上均取得最高PSNR（33.56 dB对比ClipGS的32.34 dB），渲染速度超过650 FPS（远超实时，对比278 FPS）；在体素轴切面视图中，平均带SSIM从0.809提升至0.860，泄漏减少约40倍；无需重新训练即可在任意法向平面上取得四项指标的平均最佳；在固定内部上与RaRa的面保真度相当，且泄漏减少约16倍。

Conclusion: XClipGS通过闭式精确裁剪算子与针对性内部监督协议，在保持实时交互渲染的同时显著提升了裁剪切面的渲染质量与物理保真度，为医学体积可视化提供了一种更精确、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Gaussian-splatting proxies enable interactive rendering of volumetric medical scans, but a clipping plane exposes anatomy not constrained by external-view training and intersects primitives that conventional splatting can only keep or drop whole. We present XClipGS (eXact Clipping), which treats these as two separate problems: the render-time clip operator and supervision of the hidden interior. Under the local affine model used by EWA splatting, the ray integral of a half-space-restricted Gaussian factorizes exactly into its ordinary 2D footprint and a conditional Gaussian CDF whose argument is affine in pixel coordinates. The resulting closed-form per-pixel operator introduces no learned clipping parameters or auxiliary network and remains differentiable with respect to the primitive and plane. We use multi-distance reference views with varied clipping-plane axes and offsets to supervise the interior through the same operator. We also introduce a paired clipped/unclipped cut-face protocol with difference-referenced cut error (CDE) and culled-side leakage (Leak), because global image metrics dilute errors near the plane. On eight CT and MRI volumes with plane offsets not used for training, XClipGS attains the highest PSNR on every volume (33.56 versus 32.34 dB for ClipGS) while rendering at over 650 FPS, far above real time, versus 278 FPS. On voxel-axis cut-face views, it raises average band SSIM from 0.809 to 0.860 and leaks roughly 40 times less. Without retraining, it also achieves the best average across all four metrics on arbitrary-normal planes; on a fixed interior, it matches RaRa's face fidelity with about 16 times less leakage. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/XClipGS/

</details>

*Jiayang Lu,Fengming Lin,Alejandro F. Frangi,Ali Sarrami-Foroushani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DINO-3DRA是一个双路径框架，通过将冻结的DINOv3二维视觉基础模型特征注入3D U-Net骨干网络（使用Room-Lite空间混合和校准残差融合），实现有效的跨维度语义迁移，在3D旋转血管造影动脉瘤分割中达到SOTA性能（Dice:0.758, HD95:2.75mm，较nnU-Net提升13%），且仅需5.72M可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DRA中的动脉瘤分割面临极端类别不平衡、与血管形态相似以及缺乏大规模3D预训练三大挑战。虽然2D视觉基础模型编码了大量密集结构先验，但简单的逐切片迁移会破坏解剖连续性并导致优化不稳定，因此需要一种有效的跨维度语义迁移方法。

Method: 提出DINO-3DRA双路径框架：将冻结的DINOv3特征通过Room-Lite空间混合机制注入3D U-Net骨干网络，并采用校准残差融合实现跨二维到三维的特征桥接，在保持3D解剖连续性的同时利用2D基础模型的先验知识。

Result: 在多中心3DRA数据上达到SOTA效果：Dice 0.758、HD95 2.75mm，较nnU-Net提升13%。消融实验证实性能提升来源于结构化跨维度迁移而非仅损失函数设计。在CADA和SHINY-ICARUS上无需微调即消除了基线架构的全部灾难性失败案例，展现出跨异构成像协议的鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: DINO-3DRA证明将2D基础模型的先验知识通过精心设计的跨维度融合机制迁移到3D医学影像分割是可行的，在极低参数量下实现了优异性能与稳健泛化，为克服3D医学影像缺乏大规模预训练的困境提供了新思路。

Abstract: Accurate aneurysm segmentation in 3D rotational angiography (3DRA) is hindered by extreme class imbalance, morphological similarity to vessels, and absent large-scale 3D pretraining. 2D vision foundation models encode dense structural priors from 1.7 billion images, yet naïve slice-wise transfer fragments anatomical continuity and destabilises optimisation. We propose DINO-3DRA, a dual-path framework achieving effective cross-dimensional semantic transfer by injecting frozen DINOv3 features into a 3D U-Net backbone via Room-Lite spatial mixing and calibrated residual fusion. On multi-centre 3DRA data, DINO-3DRA achieves state-of-the-art aneurysm segmentation (Dice: 0.758; HD95: 2.75 mm; +13% over nnU-Net) with only 5.72M trainable parameters. Ablation studies confirm that gains arise from structured cross-dimensional transfer rather than loss design alone, with bridged foundation features improving anatomical continuity between aneurysms and parent vessels. Without fine-tuning on CADA and SHINY-ICARUS, DINO-3DRA eliminates all catastrophic failure cases observed in baseline architectures, demonstrating robust generalisation across heterogeneous imaging protocols.

</details>


### [44] [SeqLoc: Beyond the Single Frame for Cross-View Geo-Localization in Feature-Sparse Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07835)
*Junwei Zheng,Yun Huang,Ruize Dai,Ruiping Liu,Yufan Chen,Kunyu Peng,Kailun Yang,Jiaming Zhang,Guangming Wang,Olaf Wysocki,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对跨视角地理定位（CVGL）在特征稀疏场景（如乡村道路）中性能崩溃的问题，提出了CV-FSS基准数据集和SeqLoc在线测试时序列聚合机制。SeqLoc通过熵调节不确定性、地图引导重定位和峰值锚定平滑三个关键组件，在CV-FSS和CV-RHO上相比单帧定位方法在位置和方向召回率上均提升超过50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨视角地理定位方法在结构丰富的城市环境中表现良好，但在特征稀疏的场景（如乡村道路）中会严重退化。作者希望系统性地研究这一失败模式，并设计更鲁棒的定位方法。

Method: 提出了SeqLoc，一种在线测试时序列聚合机制，包含三个关键组件：（1）熵调节不确定性（ETU），通过归一化熵对每个到来的姿态似然体进行调节；（2）地图引导重定位（MGR），将地图形状的恢复分布混合到信念中，使被抑制的真实位姿能够恢复；（3）峰值锚定平滑（PAS），在子网格精度下推导最终位姿。

Result: 在CV-FSS和CV-RHO基准上的大量实验表明，SeqLoc相比单帧定位方法有大幅提升，位置和方向召回率均提高了超过50%。

Conclusion: 序列聚合能够有效缓解跨视角地理定位在特征稀疏场景下的性能崩溃问题，且该方法在多个基准上均表现出显著的鲁棒性提升。基准和源代码将公开。

Abstract: Cross-View Geo-Localization (CVGL) with OpenStreetMap (OSM) performs well in structure-rich urban environments but collapses in feature-sparse scenes such as rural roads. To study this failure mode, in this work, we introduce CV-FSS, a benchmark that pairs sequential panoramas from five rural regions with aligned OSM maps, on which single-frame methods degrade drastically. We then propose SeqLoc, an online test-time sequence aggregation mechanism that recursively maintains a log-belief volume with three key components: (1) Entropy-Tempered Uncertainty (ETU) tempers each incoming pose likelihood volume by its normalized entropy; (2) Map-Guided Relocalization (MGR) mixes a map-shaped recovery distribution into the belief so that a suppressed true pose can recover; (3) Peak-Anchored Smoothing (PAS) derives the final pose at sub-grid precision. Extensive experiments on CV-FSS and CV-RHO demonstrate that SeqLoc outperforms single-frame localization by a large margin, improving both position and orientation recall by over 50%. The benchmark and source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [45] [IRPol-Fuse: Energy-structure coordination for infrared polarization fusion under low visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07848)
*Zhuangfan Huang,Chusheng Fang,Xiaosong Li,Yang Liua,Xiaoqi Cheng,Haishu Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IRPol-Fuse框架，通过能量-结构协调策略解决低能见度下红外-偏振图像融合中弱偏振纹理被抑制的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外-偏振图像融合方法过度强调红外主导响应，导致暗区中微弱但信息丰富的偏振纹理被压制，影响低能见度场景下的感知质量。

Method: 提出IRPol-Fuse框架，包含三个关键模块：偏振注意力融合（自适应红外-偏振分配）、红外高光注入器（高亮引导的红外保留）和偏振纹理注入器（偏振纹理恢复与细节重建）；同时构建了LI-PI专用评估数据集。

Result: 在LI-PI和公开的LDDRS数据集上，IRPol-Fuse在热目标保留、结构细节恢复和视觉自然度方面均取得良好表现；区域感知评估和下游目标检测验证了能量-结构协调策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 能量-结构协调策略能够同时保留红外目标显著性信息和偏振结构信息，在实际低能见度场景中具有良好应用价值。

Abstract: Robust perception under low-visibility conditions requires fused imagery that jointly preserves infrared thermal saliency and polarization-derived structural details. However, existing infrared-polarization image fusion (IPIF) methods often overemphasize dominant infrared responses, causing weak yet informative polarization textures in dark regions to be suppressed. To address this issue, we propose IRPol-Fuse, an energy-structure coordinated IPIF framework for challenging low-visibility scenarios. The proposed framework contains three key modules: Polarization Attention Fusion for adaptive infrared-polarization allocation, Infrared Highlight Injector for highlight-guided infrared preservation, and Polarization Texture Injector for polarization texture restoration and fine-detail recovery. We further construct LI-PI, a dedicated infrared-polarization evaluation dataset for low-visibility and visually concealed scenes. Experiments on LI-PI and the public LDDRS dataset demonstrate that IRPol-Fuse achieves favorable performance in thermal target preservation, structural detail recovery, and visual naturalness. Region-aware evaluation and downstream object detection further verify that the proposed energy-structure coordination strategy effectively preserves both infrared target saliency and polarization-derived structural information. Code is available at https://github.com/1hzf/IRPolar-Fuse .

</details>


### [46] [Distilling CT Foundation Models into Editable Concept Bottlenecks for Lung Nodule Malignancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07857)
*Fakrul Islam Tushar,Stephen Adamo,Geoffrey D. Rubin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于两个冻结CT基础模型表征的概念瓶颈模型，用于肺结节恶性预测。模型将CT表征映射到八个放射科医生定义的结节属性，再从概念和结节大小预测恶性。内部AUROC为0.86，外部为0.72-0.73，与单独使用结节大小相当，但通过概念可提供可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型提供的CT表征具有可迁移性，但直接基于嵌入的预测难以解释。作者希望构建可解释的恶性预测模型，使预测不仅能给出结果，还能追溯到临床可理解的概念特征。

Method: 使用两个冻结的CT基础模型（CT-FM和FMCIB）提取表征，通过八个岭回归概念头预测放射科定义的肺结节属性，再从概念和结节大小预测恶性。概念保真度用五折交叉验证R²评估，恶性判别用AUROC及患者分组bootstrap置信区间评估。

Result: FMCIB在概念保真度上优于CT-FM（如细微度R² 0.24 vs 0.11，毛刺征0.17 vs 0.08）。内部测试AUROC均为0.86，外部为0.72-0.73，与单独结节大小的0.73相当，优于仅嵌入探针的0.60和0.67。

Conclusion: 概念瓶颈模型提供了透明的恶性预测，判别能力与结节大小单独使用相当。概念保真度的差异表明概念恢复能力取决于底层基础模型的表征方式。

Abstract: Foundation models provide transferable CT representations, but predictions based directly on these embeddings are difficult to interpret. We developed concept bottleneck models that map two frozen CT foundation-model representations to eight radiologist-defined pulmonary-nodule attributes and predict malignancy from the estimated concepts and nodule size. The models included CT-FM, a whole-CT self-supervised encoder using a 96^3-voxel nodule-centered patch, and FMCIB, a nodule-focused contrastive encoder using a 50-mm crop. Eight ridge-regression concept heads were trained on 2,610 LIDC-IDRI nodules. Malignancy models were trained on LUNA25 and evaluated on a held-out internal test set and the external DLCS cohort. Concept fidelity was assessed using five-fold cross-validated R^2, and malignancy discrimination was assessed using AUROC with 95% confidence intervals estimated by patient-grouped bootstrap resampling. Concept fidelity was modest but higher for FMCIB than CT-FM for subtlety (R2, 0.24 vs. 0.11), spiculation (0.17 vs. 0.08), texture (0.17 vs. 0.07), and lobulation (0.15 vs. 0.05). Internally, the CT-FM and FMCIB concept+size models achieved AUROCs of 0.86 (95% CI, 0.80-0.92) and 0.86 (0.79-0.92), respectively. Externally, AUROCs were 0.72 (0.68-0.75) and 0.73 (0.70-0.76), compared with 0.73 for nodule size alone and 0.60 and 0.67 for the corresponding embedding only probes. Additive predictions could be decomposed into feature-level contributions and modified through controlled concept interventions. Concept bottlenecks provided transparent malignancy predictions with discrimination similar to nodule size alone, while differences in concept fidelity suggest that concept recovery depends on the underlying foundation-model representation.

</details>


### [47] [How Much Does It Cost to Answer My Question? Benchmarking Cloud VLM-based VQA Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07861)
*Henri Vanhuynegem,Weitao Xu,Yiran Shen,Guohao Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VQABench，首个系统性地将客户端输入预处理作为可控变量来评估云VLM驱动的移动视觉问答系统的基准。通过对3个VQA数据集、4个商业VLM、12种预处理技术的95,168次API调用，揭示了预处理并非普遍有益，其效果取决于目标模型、API范式、提供商的token计费规则和任务形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备上运行VLM困难，VQA系统普遍将推理卸载到云端商业VLM，但客户端图像预处理成为关键优化变量。然而预处理技术的成本-质量影响从未在商业云VLM上被系统研究，开发者缺乏指导来选择适当的预处理策略。

Method: 构建VQABench基准，系统地评估12种预处理技术，涵盖3个VQA数据集和来自3个提供商的4种商业VLM，总计进行95,168次API调用，从答案质量、payload大小、token成本、系统延迟等多个维度综合衡量预处理的效果。

Result: 预处理并非普遍有益，其有效性取决于目标模型、API范式、提供商token计费规则和任务形式。选择不当的预处理策略会增加部署成本或延迟，同时降低答案准确率。

Conclusion: VQABench阐明了预处理何时有帮助、何时失效及其原因，为移动VQA系统的实际部署和未来研究提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are becoming a practical backend for mobile visual question answering (VQA) systems, enabling smartphones and smart glasses to answer users' questions about the physical world. Since modern VLMs remain difficult to run on mobile and edge devices, VQA systems increasingly offload inference to cloud-based VLMs. This gives mobile devices access to stronger computation, but it also makes visual input preparation a key system variable: how the image is prepared before offloading affects not only answer quality but also payload size, token cost, and system latency. Proprietary APIs expose little control over model internals or serving behavior, leaving client-side preprocessing as the main practical optimization space for downstream developers. Many such techniques have been proposed for visual offloading, yet their cost-quality impact on commercial cloud VLMs has never been studied. To fill this gap, we present VQABench, the first systematic benchmark that treats client-side input preprocessing as a controlled variable for cloud-VLM-based VQA. We evaluate 12 preprocessing techniques across three VQA datasets and four commercial VLMs from three providers, totaling 95,168 API calls. Our results show that preprocessing is not universally beneficial: its effectiveness depends on the target model, API paradigm, provider token-accounting rule, and task formulation. A poorly selected preprocessing strategy can increase deployment cost or latency while degrading answer accuracy. Overall, our benchmark clarifies when preprocessing helps, when it fails, and why, providing insights to guide future research and real-world deployment of VQA systems.

</details>


### [48] [LHSDet: High-Resolution AI-Generated Image Detection via Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07863)
*Qian Yao,Jun-Jie Huang,Yongjun Wang,Luming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LHSDet，一种面向高分辨率AI生成图像检测的新方法。该方法将检测任务建模为视觉问答问题，采用三分支架构（低层视觉分支、高层视觉分支、语义文本分支）分别提取局部纹理、全局感知和语义特征，实现多模态融合检测。实验表明其在扩散模型和自回归模型生成的图像上均具有高检测精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI生成图像检测方法通常需要对图像进行下采样，这会导致高分辨率图像中关键的底层纹理细节被忽略，从而限制检测性能；同时，新兴生成模型的不断涌现使得大规模预训练数据集难以获取。这两个挑战促使作者探索新的检测框架。

Method: LHSDet将AI生成图像检测任务形式化为视觉问答（VQA）问题，采用微调后的视觉-语言框架。具体包含三个分支：（1）低层视觉分支，通过聚合非重叠图像块提取局部纹理线索；（2）高层视觉分支，基于SigLIP2提取全局感知特征；（3）语义级文本分支，利用BLIP-2生成图像描述。三支特征进行多模态融合并用于检测。

Result: 大量实验结果表明，LHSDet在多种生成模型（包括扩散模型和自回归模型）生成的图像上均取得了高检测精度和鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: LHSDet通过三分支多模态架构有效解决了高分辨率AI生成图像检测中因下采样导致的纹理细节丢失问题，并利用视觉-语言框架克服了未知生成模型带来的数据不可获取挑战，为AI生成图像检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Driven by advances in diffusion models and autoregressive models, the fidelity and resolution of AI-generated images now rival those of real images. However, existing AI-generated image detection methods often downsample the images, inevitably overlooking critical low-level texture details in high-resolution AI-generated images, therefore limiting their detection performance. In addition, the ceaseless emergence of unknown generative models makes large-scale pre-training datasets inaccessible. To address these challenges, we propose a novel high-resolution AI-generated image detector, termed LHSDet. Specifically, we formulate the AI-generated image detection task as a Visual Question Answering problem, leveraging a fine-tuned vision-language framework to fully exploit the complementary information between visual and textual modalities. Recognizing that the default visual encoder of existing vision-language models is not tailored for AI-generated image detection, we redesign a visual encoder to better capture both the low-level and high-level artifacts inherent in AI-generated images. Furthermore, we incorporate a semantic-level textual branch to enable multi-modal feature fusion and detection. Consequently, LHSDet employs a triple-branch architecture to extract complementary multi-modal features: a low-level visual branch that aggregates non-overlapping patches for local texture cues, a high-level visual branch based on SigLIP2 for global perception feature extraction, and a semantic-level textual branch that generates captions using BLIP-2. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that LHSDet achieves high detection accuracy and robust performance across diverse generative models, including both diffusion and autoregressive models.

</details>


### [49] [UniScale: Arbitrary-Scale Industrial Anomaly Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07864)
*Shilei Zeng,Linxin Guan,Xurui Li,Yaohan Tang,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniScale，一个统一的多尺度高保真工业异常生成框架。通过错误抑制多尺度训练（EMT）策略和生成后融合去噪（Generation-then-Fusion Denoising）推理方法，解决了扩散模型在小尺度异常生成中因极端降采样导致信息丢失的问题。实验表明该方法在异常生成质量和下游检测性能上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测面临真实异常样本稀缺的挑战，虽然生成模型可用于创建异常数据，但现有方法在处理小尺度异常时效果不佳。其根本原因是扩散模型中的极端降采样导致小异常信息在潜空间中丢失。

Method: UniScale包含两大核心组件：1）训练阶段采用错误抑制多尺度训练（EMT）策略，使模型学习丰富的位置感知异常纹理，同时抑制上采样引起的插值误差，确保既能学习小尺度异常又能保持常规尺度异常的效果；2）推理阶段采用生成后融合去噪（Generation-then-Fusion Denoising），将异常生成与背景融合解耦，防止小异常被淹没。

Result: 在VisA上IS(a)相对提升45.86%（从1.81到2.64），在MVTec AD 2上提升37.70%（从1.22到1.68）；下游像素级IoU在VisA上提升4.22%，在MVTec AD 2上AUROC提升6.55%。

Conclusion: UniScale在任意尺度的工业异常生成上实现了高保真效果，显著优于现有方法，同时在下游异常检测任务中也带来了实质性提升，并已开源代码。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly inspection faces a major challenge due to the lack of real-world anomaly samples. While generative models are used to create anomaly data, existing methods still struggle when handling small-scale anomalies.This failure occurs because extreme downsampling in diffusion models causes the information of small anomalies to be lost in the latent space.To address this, we introduce UniScale, a unified training and inference framework for high-fidelity industrial anomaly generation across arbitrary scales.During training, we introduce an Error-Suppressed Multi-Scale Training (EMT) strategy, which enables the model to learn the rich location-aware textures of anomalies, while suppressing upsampling-induced interpolation errors in texture acquisition, ensuring the model is capable of learning small-scale anomalies, while remaining effective for regular scale anomalies.For inference, we propose Generation-then-Fusion Denoising. It decouples anomaly generation from background integration, preventing small anomalies from being overwhelmed.Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art competitors in both anomaly generation quality and downstream detection performance.It achieves a relative IS(a) improvement of 45.86\% (from 1.81 to 2.64) on VisA and 37.70\% (from 1.22 to 1.68) on MVTec AD 2, while also improving the downstream pixel-level IoU by 4.22\% on VisA and AUROC by 6.55\% on MVTec AD 2. Code is available at https://github.com/HUST-SLOW/UniScale.

</details>


### [50] [Vision-Language Grounding as Bidirectional Concept Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07886)
*Jieyu Zhang,Ziqi Gao,Luke Zettlemoyer,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将视觉-语言定位重新定义为"双向概念对应"任务，即同时进行文本分割、图像分割和跨模态对齐，无需预先给定文本短语。作者提出ConCor-1模型，利用可学习的桥接token来预测文本掩码、图像掩码和对应存在分数，在长字幕数据集上对应F1提升48%，在零样本LVIS上提升29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言定位方法大多假设文本短语或类别名称已预先给定，仅解决单向定位问题，忽视了接地通信中更基本的挑战：确定文本中哪些部分是视觉可指称的，以及它们如何与图像中的实体对应。

Method: 将接地任务形式化为双向概念对应问题，统一了短语定位、指称表达定位和开放词汇检测。提出ConCor-1模型，基于预训练视觉-语言模型构建，使用可学习的桥接token表示候选图像-文本对应关系，为每个token预测文本掩码、图像掩码和对应存在分数。将多种接地和分割数据集转换为统一的对应格式进行训练和评估。

Result: ConCor-1在长字幕数据集上对应F1比基线提升48%，在零样本LVIS（以大型类别列表作为文本输入）上提升29%，持续优于各类基线方法。

Conclusion: 将视觉-语言定位重新定义为双向概念对应任务，消除了对预先给定文本跨度假设的限制，且实验表明该方法在多个设置下均显著优于基线，为更全面的视觉-语言接地提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language grounding connects language to visual content, yet most existing formulations reduce grounding to a unidirectional localization problem: given a prespecified text phrase or category name, identify the corresponding image region. This setup assumes that the relevant linguistic unit is already known, overlooking a more basic challenge in grounded communication: determining which parts of the text are visually referential and how they correspond to entities in the image. We formulate grounding as $\textit{bidirectional concept correspondence}$ over an image-text pair. Given an image and its paired text, the goal is to recover all correspondences between visually referential text spans and instance-level image segments, without assuming that the relevant text spans are provided. This formulation unifies common grounding tasks, including phrase grounding, referring expression grounding, and open-vocabulary detection, by treating text segmentation, image segmentation, and cross-modal alignment as a single correspondence prediction problem. To address this task, we introduce $\textbf{ConCor-1}$, a grounding model built on top of a pretrained vision-language model. It uses learnable $\textit{bridge tokens}$ to represent candidate image-text correspondences and predicts, for each token, a text mask, an image mask, and a correspondence presence score. To train and evaluate this task, we convert diverse grounding and segmentation datasets into a unified correspondence format. Experiments show that $\textbf{ConCor-1}$ consistently outperforms baselines, improving correspondence F1 by 48% on the long-caption dataset and by 29% on zero-shot LVIS, where the large category list serves as the text input.

</details>


### [51] [DeCo: Zero-Shot Industrial Anomaly Generation through Decoupling and Recoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07904)
*Shilei Zeng,Xurui Li,Yaohan Tang,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了DeCo方法用于零样本工业异常生成，通过解耦异常结构与源产品、再与目标产品正常纹理重耦合并显式融合，解决了现有方法中异常信息获取不准确和异常-产品融合不可控的问题，在MVTec AD和VisA上分别提升5.1%和8.2%的像素AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测受限于真实异常数据稀缺，零样本异常生成虽可针对特定产品生成异常而不需要真实异常图像，但现有方法存在两个关键局限：异常信息获取不准确以及异常-产品融合过程不可控。

Method: 提出DeCo方法，将异常结构与源产品解耦，再与目标产品的正常纹理显式重耦合。具体包含两个模块：(1) 异常信息获取阶段：Dual-Routing Flow (DR-Flow) 将纹理不变的异常结构绑定到异常token，同时Product-Invariant Flow (PI-Flow) 作为并行约束防止异常token绑定到源产品；(2) 异常-产品融合阶段：采用混合注入将获取的异常结构与目标产品重耦合，并引入Product Compatibility Correction (PCC) 补偿异常结构与产品之间的不兼容性。

Result: 大量实验表明DeCo达到了新的最先进水平，在下游检测模型训练中，MVTec AD上像素AP提升了5.1%，VisA上提升了8.2%。

Conclusion: DeCo通过解耦-重耦合的显式设计有效解决了零样本工业异常生成中信息获取不准确和融合不可控的问题，为工业异常检测提供了高质量的训练数据，代码已开源。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly inspection is severely hindered by the scarcity of real anomalous data.Zero-shot industrial anomaly generation addresses this by generating anomalies on specific products without requiring any of their real anomalous images. However, existing methods suffer from two critical limitations, i.e., inaccurate anomaly information acquisition and uncontrolled anomaly-product fusion.To overcome these challenges, we propose DeCo, which decouples the anomaly structure from its source product, and explicitly recouples it with the normal textures of the target product. During anomaly information acquisition,Dual-Routing Flow (DR-Flow) binds the texture-invariant anomaly structure to an abnormal token, while a parallel constraint, Product-Invariant Flow (PI-Flow), prevents the abnormal token from binding the source product. During anomaly-product fusion, we propose a hybrid injection to recouple the acquired anomaly structure with the target product, and Product Compatibility Correction (PCC) to compensate for the incompatibility between the acquired anomaly structure and the product. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeCo establishes a new state-of-the-art. Training downstream detection models on our generated data yields massive pixel AP improvements of 5.1\% on MVTec AD and 8.2\% on VisA. Code is available at https://github.com/HUST-SLOW/DeCo.

</details>


### [52] [SegDem: Segmentation helps Demosaicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07916)
*Ping Chen,Xiangming Wang,Yongyong Chen,Jiezhang Cao,Kai Zhang,Jingyong Su,Jie Liu,Haijin Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SegDem，一种基于跨任务解码器表示迁移的图像去马赛克框架。核心思想是重构与视觉理解是共享场景结构的互补视角，通过实例分割预训练学习区域和边界感知表示，然后将解码器迁移到RAW条件下的重构任务，并锚定到冻结的DINOv2表示空间以保持结构一致性。该方法在卷积、Transformer和状态空间骨干网络上均取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像去马赛克方法大多将其视为像素级重构问题，主要依赖局部纹理、跨通道相关性和低级图像统计信息，忽视了视觉理解与重构任务之间的互补关系。作者认为两者根植于同一物理世界，其推断的结构和物理信息应保持一致，因此引入实例分割的结构信息来辅助去马赛克。

Method: SegDem框架包含三个阶段：(1) 通过实例感知结构预训练学习区域和边界感知表示；(2) 将解码器迁移到RAW条件下的重构任务；(3) 将分割和去马赛克条件特征锚定到共享的冻结DINOv2表示空间，以保持跨任务的结构组织。支持卷积、Transformer和状态空间三种骨干网络，统一处理单Bayes和四Bayes去马赛克。

Result: 在合成、外部和具有挑战性的数据集上的大量实验表明，SegDem在不同架构和CFA布局上均取得一致性能提升，验证了跨任务结构信息迁移的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了视觉理解和图像重构可以互为辅助，通过跨任务表示迁移能够显著提升去马赛克性能。用跨任务解码器表示迁移的创新框架，为低级视觉任务引入高级语义信息提供了新思路，并在多种骨干网络和CFA布局上得到验证。

Abstract: Image demosaicing reconstructs a full-color image from incomplete color measurements produced by a sensor covered with a color filter array (CFA). Most existing methods formulate demosaicing as pixel-level reconstruction and mainly rely on local textures, cross-channel correlations, and low-level image statistics. Our core insight is that reconstruction and visual understanding can be viewed as complementary views of shared scene structure: both are grounded in the same underlying physical world, and therefore the structural and physical information inferred from an image should remain consistent across the two tasks. We instantiate this idea with instance segmentation and propose \emph{SegDem}, a cross-task decoder representation transfer framework for demosaicing. SegDem first learns region- and boundary-aware representations through instance-aware structural pretraining and then transfers the decoder to RAW-conditioned reconstruction. Segmentation- and demosaicing-conditioned features are further anchored to a shared frozen DINOv2 representation space to preserve structural organization across tasks. We instantiate SegDem with convolutional, Transformer-based, and state-space backbones for unified Single- and Quad-Bayer demosaicing. Extensive experiments on synthetic, external, and challenging datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across different architectures and CFA layouts.

</details>


### [53] [Forged Peer Judgments Mislead Multimodal LLM Judge Panels: Source-Blind Anchoring and Panel-Consensus Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07920)
*Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多模态LLM评审小组中的"来源盲锚定"攻击面：引用同行的独立视觉判断会产生19-26个百分点的锚定缺口，且标签本身并不解释该效应。恶意生成的错误引用比自然错误同行陈述更能推翻正确裁决（1.5-2.7倍）。作者提出"面板共识验证"防御方法，可阻止84.9%的伪造攻击并将净危害降低97.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM评审小组依靠同行引用来交叉验证，但被引用的同行判断本身可能不被信任。当前缺乏对"来源盲锚定"这一文本级攻击面的系统性研究，需要识别这一低成本攻击面并提出防御措施，以保障多模态协作评估的安全性。

Method: 通过设计对照实验来分离锚定效应：使用匹配内容但仅标签不同的对照（self/peer标签），测量锚定缺口；比较自然错误同行陈述与刻意生成的错误引用的影响差异；并提出面板共识验证方法，将引用与独立收集的盲投票进行交叉验证。

Result: 引用独立视觉判断产生19-26个百分点的锚定差距；标签对照仅改变-0.17pp的失败率，证明标签本身不解释效应；恶意生成的错误引用是自然错误陈述的1.5-2.7倍，能推翻原本正确的裁决；面板共识验证阻止了84.9%的伪造攻击，净危害降低97.5%。

Conclusion: 来源盲锚定是一个低成本且可利用的攻击面，严重影响VLM评审小组的可信度。面板共识验证是一种有效的防御措施，能大幅削弱该攻击的影响，并保持对真实同行信息的估值。研究为多模态协作评估提供了具体的安全改进方向。

Abstract: Multimodal LLM judge panels can cross-reference peers, but a quoted peer judgment may itself be untrusted. We expose source-blind anchoring as a text-level attack surface in vision-language model (VLM) panels. Quoting independent visual judgments creates large anchoring gaps (19--26 percentage points) under both self and peer framing. A matched-content, label-only control changes the broken rate by only $-0.17$pp (95\% CI $[-0.68,0.35]$), showing that the self/peer label itself does not explain the effect. Under our tested construction, deliberately generated, concise wrong quotes overturn originally-correct verdicts 1.5--2.7$\times$ more often than naturally occurring wrong peer statements, with bootstrap 95\% CIs excluding parity across two datasets and seven VLM judges. Because the two statement populations differ in selection and form, this ratio measures differential damage under the tested attack rather than a provenance-only causal effect. We then introduce panel-consensus verification, which cross-checks a quote against independently collected blind votes. It blocks 84.9\% of fabricated attacks, cuts their net harm by 97.5\%, and preserves the positive but statistically inconclusive point estimate for genuine peer information under leave-one-out re-verification. These results identify a low-cost attack surface and a concrete defense for safer multimodal collaborative evaluation.

</details>


### [54] [SCoPE: Training-Free Audio-Visual Event Perception via Sparse Cross-Modal Prior Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07923)
*Jaemo Jeong,Junho Yoon,Hyunju Kim,Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCoPE，一个无需训练的框架，通过让所有查询标签竞争共享证据、并让模态间互相引导事件选择，从而解决音视频事件感知中"虚假共激活"（FCA）问题，在多个基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在音视频事件感知（AVEP）中，训练无关方法通过匹配冻结的音频和视觉特征与文本编码事件名称来查询新事件词汇。但相关标签共享证据，导致错误标签得分可能不低于正确标签（称为虚假共激活FCA），且无法通过标量阈值区分正确与错误标签。

Method: 提出SCoPE框架：所有查询标签共享证据进行竞争，每个模态引导另一模态的事件选择。并推导了在双标签拟合中移除FCA的精确条件。

Result: 使用相同的冻结CLIP+CLAP骨干网络，在LLP数据集上相比报告值，Type@seg提升7.45个点，Event@seg提升5.04个点。同一配置可无修改迁移到OV-AVEBench和VGGSound-AVEL100k。

Conclusion: SCoPE通过标签竞争与跨模态引导机制，在无需训练的条件下有效消除FCA问题，并在多个基准上取得显著且可迁移的性能提升。

Abstract: Audio-visual event perception (AVEP) determines which events occur in a video, when they occur, and whether they are audible, visible, or both. Training-free methods query new event vocabularies by matching frozen audio and visual features with text-encoded event names. However, related labels share evidence. An incorrect label can then score at least as high as a correct one. We call this a false co-activation (FCA). No scalar cutoff can reject the incorrect label while keeping every correct one. Class-specific thresholds may prevent that label from becoming a final prediction, but the FCA remains in the underlying score vector. We introduce SCoPE, a training-free framework in which all queried labels compete for shared evidence and each modality guides event selection in the other. We derive an exact condition for when this competition removes an FCA in a two-label fit. With identical frozen CLIP+CLAP backbones on LLP, SCoPE improves Type@seg by 7.45 points and Event@seg by 5.04 points compared with the reported AV$^2$A values. The same fixed configuration transfers unchanged to OV-AVEBench and VGGSound-AVEL100k.

</details>


### [55] [SportsGrounder: Proposal-Aided Interleaved Grounding for Dense Sports Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07932)
*Yizhi Li,Jiawei Jiang,Guanhong Wang,Yingcai Wu,Gaoang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SportsGrounder框架，利用开放词汇视觉专家辅助交错式grounding，解决稠密体育视频推理中LMM对小尺度、高交互、视觉同质实体（如运动员、球）理解不足的问题。通过Interleaved Grounding Fusion机制融合边界框坐标与视觉语义，结合Action-Aware Supervision正则化隐状态，并使用Mixed Preference Optimization训练，在SoccerNet和FineSports衍生数据集上达到SOTA精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型多模态模型（LMMs）在稠密体育视频推理中因缺乏细粒度视觉细节，过度依赖文本先验猜测答案，尤其是在区分视觉相似的运动员和动作时表现不佳，亟需一种能精准空间定位并建模细粒度动作表示的方法。

Method: 1) 利用开放词汇视觉专家提取领域引导的目标提议；2) 提出Interleaved Grounding Fusion (IGF)机制，逐帧融合显式边界框坐标和隐式视觉语义与全局网格特征，保持严格时间对齐并防止序列长度爆炸；3) 设计Action-Aware Supervision (AAS)模块直接正则化模型隐状态，强制网络学习准确的动作表示；4) 使用Mixed Preference Optimization (MPO)优化以更好区分欺骗性干扰项。

Result: 在新构建的稠密体育VQA数据集（源自SoccerNet和FineSports）上的大量实验表明，SportsGrounder显著提升细粒度推理能力，达到最先进的准确率。

Conclusion: SportsGrounder通过交错式grounding融合、动作感知监督和混合偏好优化，有效解决了LMM在稠密体育视频推理中依赖语言偏置、缺乏细粒度视觉理解的问题，验证了显式空间定位对稠密运动场景推理的重要性。

Abstract: Sports video analysis is crucial for athletic analytics and broadcasting enhancement. Dense sports video reasoning, however, demands a fine-grained understanding of numerous small-scale, highly interactive, and visually homogeneous entities (e.g., players sharing identical uniforms, the ball) across long temporal contexts. Current Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) inherently struggle with such dense visual complexities. Due to the lack of fine-grained visual details, these models often over-rely on textual priors to guess answers, especially when distinguishing visually similar actions and players. To address this, we propose \textbf{SportsGrounder}, a framework that leverages an open-vocabulary visual expert to aid interleaved grounding specifically for dense sports video reasoning. To achieve precise spatial localization, we extract domain-guided object proposals and introduce an Interleaved Grounding Fusion (IGF) mechanism. The IGF frame-by-frame integrates explicit bounding box coordinates and implicit visual semantics with global grid features. This design preserves strict temporal alignment and prevents sequence length explosion. Furthermore, we design an Action-Aware Supervision (AAS) module that directly regularizes the model's hidden states, forcing the network to learn accurate motion representations rather than relying on language bias. Optimized with Mixed Preference Optimization (MPO) to better distinguish deceptive distractors, our extensive experiments on newly curated dense sports VQA datasets (derived from SoccerNet and FineSports) demonstrate that SportsGrounder significantly improves fine-grained reasoning and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy.

</details>


### [56] [FlexSplat: Flexible Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting without Point Cloud Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07937)
*Amir Sabbaghziarani,Hanting Ye,Maria Gorlatova,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexSplat是一个前馈式的新视角合成框架，无需相机位姿标定即可从多视图图像集合重建3D高斯场景。通过联合训练几何transformer与高斯解码器，预测每图像的相机参数和深度，实现校准无关的查询式高斯重建，在ShapeNet-SRN和GSO上接近甚至匹配有姿态标注的最先进方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于查询的方法（如通过多视角可变形注意力精炼3D高斯查询）依赖已知相机位姿，这限制了实际应用。作者旨在去除这一假设，使查询式高斯重建能够在无标定条件下工作，同时保持紧凑且与输入分辨率解耦的高斯表示。

Method: FlexSplat包含一个几何transformer，与高斯解码器联合训练，用于预测每图像的相机参数和深度。深度引导的高斯参数化用于几何建模；多视角可变形交叉注意力跨所有输入视图聚合证据产生单一视图一致的图元集；采用不确定性加权的深度一致性目标使联合训练的几何适应重建任务，解码阶段的跨视图共识吸收相机与深度估计的残余误差。

Result: 在ShapeNet-SRN和GSO数据集上，FlexSplat匹配或接近有姿态标注的最先进重建方法，不需要相机位姿或真实深度。在GSO上LPIPS（感知质量）排名最佳，与有姿态方法相比PSNR差距在0.7 dB以内。

Conclusion: 联合训练的几何前端足以赋予查询式高斯重建校准无关的操作能力，在保证紧凑高斯预算和分辨率解耦的同时，性能接近有姿态监督的先进方法。这表明无需额外标定信息的端到端重建是可行的。

Abstract: We present FlexSplat, a feed-forward framework for novel view synthesis (NVS) from uncalibrated, object-centric multi-view image collections. A recent line of query-based methods reconstructs a compact set of 3D Gaussians by treating them as transformer queries that are refined with multi-view deformable attention; these methods, however, assume that camera poses are given. FlexSplat removes this assumption: a geometry transformer is trained jointly with the Gaussian decoder to predict per-image camera parameters and depth, which in turn ground a depth-guided Gaussian parameterization and a multi-view deformable cross-attention that aggregates evidence across all input views into a single, view-consistent set of primitives. An uncertainty-weighted depth-consistency objective lets the jointly trained geometry adapt to the reconstruction task, while the cross-view consensus formed during decoding absorbs the residual error of the estimated cameras and depth. The representation uses a compact Gaussian budget that is decoupled from the input resolution - unlike pixel-aligned methods, the primitive count does not grow with the image grid - and is not dictated by the number of views. On ShapeNet-SRN and Google Scanned Objects (GSO), FlexSplat matches or approaches posed state-of-the-art reconstructors while requiring neither camera poses nor ground-truth depth, and matches the best perceptual (LPIPS) quality among the compared methods on GSO. Our results indicate that a jointly trained geometry front-end is sufficient to bring calibration-free operation to query-based Gaussian reconstruction while staying within 0.7 dB PSNR of posed methods and matching their perceptual quality.

</details>


### [57] [LAD-COD: Language-Aligned Dense Perception for Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07941)
*Shangye Song,Tianzhi Zhu,Syed Ariff Syed Hesham,Xin He,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LAD-COD框架，通过语言对齐的稠密感知方法解决伪装物体检测（COD）中前景背景判别性低、边界证据弱的问题，在CAMO、COD10K和NC4K基准上全部12项数据集-指标比较中取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪装物体与其周围环境高度相似，导致低对比度边界和微弱的结构信息难以保持。现有基于大语言模型（LMM）的语言到掩码分割系统中，目标嵌入仅条件化掩码解码器，而未对需要保留低对比度边界和精细局部结构的稠密视觉特征提供显式引导，缺乏自顶向下语义与自底向上视觉特征的深度对齐。

Method: LAD-COD采用指令条件下的语义作为自顶向下引导，学习一个可训练的分层视觉分支来捕获伪装敏感纹理、边界和上下文信息，而非完全适配大型通用图像编码器。核心模块为语言对齐双重视觉融合（LADVF），将目标嵌入从稀疏提示扩展到查询patch级的语言对齐特征，并通过门控机制将其残差集成到分层特征中，实现语义引导定位与精细结构保留的平衡。

Result: 在CAMO、COD10K和NC4K三个标准数据集上，LAD-COD在全部12项数据集-指标组合比较中取得最佳报告值，验证了语言对齐稠密感知方案在伪装物体检测中的有效性。

Conclusion: LAD-COD通过将自顶向下语义目标引导与自底向上分层视觉特征对齐，有效解决了伪装场景中语义信息与视觉细节难以兼顾的问题，为语言引导的分割系统在低对比度场景下的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) aims to segment objects that exhibit high visual similarity to their surroundings, which reduces foreground-background discriminability and weakens boundary evidence across appearance, texture, and structure. Such limitations motivate the use of instruction-conditioned semantics as top-down guidance for identifying which weak visual cues are relevant to the target. Recent segmentation systems built on large multimodal models (LMMs) demonstrate this possibility through instruction-conditioned target embeddings that guide mask decoding. However, in this language-to-mask paradigm, the generated target embedding conditions mainly the mask decoder, leaving the dense visual features that must preserve low-contrast boundaries and fine local structure without explicit guidance. We propose Language-Aligned Dense perception for COD (LAD-COD), a framework that aligns top-down semantic target guidance with bottom-up hierarchical visual features. Instead of fully adapting a large generic image encoder, LAD-COD learns a trainable hierarchical visual branch that captures camouflage-sensitive texture, boundary, and contextual information. To align these features with the target embedding, LAD-COD applies Language-Aligned Dual Visual Fusion (LADVF), which extends the embedding beyond sparse prompting to query patch-level language-aligned features and to gate their residual integration with the hierarchical features. This design allows semantic information to guide localization while preserving the fine structural details needed for camouflage segmentation. Experiments on CAMO, COD10K, and NC4K show that LAD-COD obtains the best reported value in all 12 dataset-metric comparisons.

</details>


### [58] [SynVAR: Synergizing Spatial and Semantic Alignment in Visual Autoregressive Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07948)
*Zhennan Chen,Tianxing Shi,Pengcheng Xu,Kepan Nan,Qian Wang,Zili Yi,Jian Yang,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SynVAR，首个针对VAR范式的训练-free增强框架，通过空间-语义协同控制策略抑制跨尺度误差传播，提升复杂场景建模质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAR虽因下一尺度预测范式广受欢迎，但在处理多对象多属性的复杂场景时存在性能瓶颈，且现有基于扩散的增强方法无法有效解决VAR中跨尺度误差传播与累积这一独特挑战。

Method: SynVAR包含三个关键组件：（1）全局引导——确保早期阶段的合理空间结构；（2）感受野约束——缓解早期阶段语义混淆；（3）高频补偿——恢复细粒度细节。三者协同实现空间-语义协作控制，且为训练-free框架。

Result: 大量定量与定性实验表明，SynVAR显著提升了VAR在复杂场景建模方面的能力。

Conclusion: SynVAR作为首个为VAR范式量身定制的训练-free增强框架，有效解决了跨尺度误差传播问题，为VAR在复杂场景下的性能提升提供了可行方案。
