Abstract: VAR has gained widespread popularity due to its next-scale prediction paradigm. However, it faces substantial performance bottlenecks when handling complex scenes with multiple objects and attributes. Existing diffusion-based enhancement methods fail to adequately address the unique challenge of cross-scale error propagation and accumulation in VAR. To this end, we propose SynVAR, the first training-free enhancement framework specifically tailored for the VAR paradigm, which introduces a spatial-semantic collaborative control strategy to effectively suppress propagation error and improve generation quality. SynVAR comprises three key components: (1) Global guidance to ensure reasonable spatial structure in the early stages, (2) Receptive field constraints to mitigate early-stage semantic confusion, (3) High-frequency compensation to recover fine-grained details. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate the significant improvements in the ability of SynVAR to enhance the VAR's capability for complex scene modeling.

</details>


### [59] [LIBAD: A Multimodal Anomaly Detection Benchmark for Li-Ion Battery Electrode Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07958)
*Wenbo Sui,Daniel Lichau,Harold Phelippeau,Zhao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LIBAD，首个面向锂离子电池电极制造的 multimodal 异常检测基准数据集，并在此基础上提出DA-Core方法，通过联合考虑特征空间覆盖率和正常特征的局部密度进行 coreset 选择，在0.05的coreset比例下将FPR95从60.4%降至54.3%，并减少43.9%的推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态工业异常检测主要针对离散产品，依赖强相关的RGB和3D观测，而连续过程制造（如电池电极生产）中模态间相关性弱、缺陷证据分布不均的挑战尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出DA-Core方法，一种基于记忆库的方法，在coreset选择过程中同时考虑特征空间覆盖率和正常特征的局部密度，使紧凑的记忆库能够更好地保留细粒度的正常变异信息。

Result: 在0.05的coreset比例下，DA-Core将FPR95从60.4%降至54.3%；在该比例下还优于最佳标准coreset结果（比例为0.20时获得），同时推理时间减少43.9%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在过程制造的异常检测方法设计中，需要同时明确考虑正常特征的数据分布以及模态之间的相互关系。

Abstract: Multimodal industrial anomaly detection has largely focused on discrete products using strongly correlated RGB and 3D observations, leaving continuous process manufacturing and weakly correlated sensing modalities underexplored. We introduce LIBAD, the first multimodal anomaly detection benchmark for Li-ion battery electrode manufacturing. Collected from real roll-to-roll production lines, LIBAD provides aligned double-sided visible-light imaging, high-resolution X-ray radiography, and inline-compatible low-resolution X-ray radiography. Electrode patches in LIBAD exhibit highly homogeneous material appearance, while defect evidence can be strong in one modality but weak or absent in another, resulting in pronounced cross-modal anomaly inconsistency. Benchmarks of representative methods under the inline-compatible visible-light and low-resolution X-ray setting exhibit limited transferability and consistently high false-positive rates. We therefore propose DA-Core, a memory-based method that jointly considers feature-space coverage and local density of normal features during coreset selection, allowing compact memory banks to better preserve fine-grained normal variations. With a coreset ratio of 0.05, DA-Core reduces FPR95 from 60.4% to 54.3% compared with standard farthest point sampling. At this ratio, DA-Core also outperforms the best standard coreset result (obtained at 0.20) while reducing inference time by 43.9%. These results suggest that both the data distribution of normal features and the modality relationship itself require explicit consideration when designing anomaly detection methods for process manufacturing.

</details>


### [60] [Distilling Physical Priors into Streaming World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07981)
*Liangliang Zhao,Junying Wang,Danni Yang,Yifan Chang,Bin Fu,Yu Qiao,Bowen Zhou,Yihao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhyS，一个三阶段框架，用于将物理先验蒸馏到流式世界模型中。通过构建120K真实世界物理交互视频数据集PhyS-120K，注入物理先验到双向14B DiT教师模型，再蒸馏为轻量1.3B因果DiT用于少步自回归流式生成，最后通过在线强化学习和时序信用路由（TCR）提升物理合理性。实验在PhysicsIQ等基准上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式世界模型的长程预测往往违反基本物理约束。现有的将预训练双向DiT蒸馏为少步因果生成器的方法存在两个根本缺陷：通用双向教师模型从面向视觉的预训练中学到的物理先验有限，且这些有限先验在双向到因果蒸馏过程中进一步损失。

Method: PhyS包含三阶段：(1)构建PhyS-120K数据集，包含120K真实物理交互视频，涵盖刚体动力学、软体变形、流体现象和相变，并标注结构化对象属性和因果状态转换；(2)通过物理感知监督微调将物理先验注入14B双向DiT教师，再蒸馏为1.3B轻量因果DiT实现少步自回归流式生成；(3)使用在线强化学习激励蒸馏模型生成物理合理的rollout，并提出时序信用路由（TCR），通过重叠时间窗口评估物理一致性并将组相对优势路由到时间对齐的去噪动作。

Result: 在PhysicsIQ上，PhyS相比Wan2.1-14B教师提升18.2%，相比Self Forcing、Rolling Forcing和Causal Forcing分别提升23.7%、14.8%和31.4%。在VideoPhy、VideoPhy2和PhyGenBench等物理感知视频基准上也取得了改进。

Conclusion: PhyS通过物理数据构建、双向教师注入和在线强化学习+时序信用路由，有效解决了流式世界模型中物理先验不足和蒸馏损失的问题，显著提升了物理一致性和流式生成质量。

Abstract: Streaming world models predict future visual states online while maintaining physically coherent dynamics over long horizons. However, their rollouts often violate basic physical constraints. A common approach distills pretrained bidirectional DiTs into few-step causal generators. However, this paradigm suffers from two fundamental limitations: generic bidirectional teachers acquire limited physical priors from visually oriented pretraining, and the limited priors suffer further loss during bidirectional-to-causal distillation. We present PhyS, a three-stage framework for distilling physical priors into streaming world models. To acquire physical priors from real-world interactions, we construct PhyS-120K, a dataset of 120K real-world physical-interaction videos spanning rigid-body dynamics, soft-body deformation, fluid phenomena, and phase transitions. Each video is annotated with structured descriptions of object properties and causal state transitions. Physics-aware supervised fine-tuning injects the physical priors into a bidirectional 14B DiT teacher, which we then distill into a lightweight 1.3B causal DiT for few-step autoregressive streaming generation. Finally, we use online reinforcement learning to incentivize the distilled model to generate physically plausible rollouts and further propose Temporal Credit Routing (TCR) to address temporal credit assignment. TCR evaluates physical consistency over overlapping temporal windows and routes the resulting group-relative advantages to temporally aligned denoising actions. On PhysicsIQ, PhyS improves the Wan2.1-14B teacher by 18.2\% and the Self Forcing, Rolling Forcing, and Causal Forcing by 23.7\%, 14.8\%, and 31.4\%, respectively. Results also improve the physics-aware video benchmarks VideoPhy, VideoPhy2, and PhyGenBench. The dataset, code, and more sample videos are available on our Project Page.

</details>


### [61] [AdaDINO: Pair-Aware In-Backbone Adaptation of Frozen DINO for Efficient Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07982)
*Xu Zhang,Xinqing Li,Jianpeng Xie,Zeshuai Zhu,Xin He,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaDINO是一种面向遥感变化检测的成对感知骨干适配框架，通过将冻结的DINO编码器与双时相交互机制结合，实现了高效且准确的变化检测。其核心组件CGLA模块在冻结块之间注入共享时间残差（相反符号）以增强真实变化响应，BSCS通过批共享通道块选择减少FFN计算量，CPGR解码器复用编码器端变化响应进行粗到细预测。在四个基准数据集上取得了竞争性或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型（如DINO）是针对单张图像表示进行预训练的，而遥感变化检测需要对双时相图像对进行推理。现有基于VFM的方法通常独立编码两幅图像，之后再进行比较，导致VFM主干网络无法感知跨时相的关系，存在预训练范式与任务需求之间的不匹配。

Method: 论文提出AdaDINO框架：1）CGLA（变化感知门控局部适配）模块在选定的冻结块后耦合双流，注入符号相反的共享时间残差，增强真实变化响应并保持对中点的一致性；2）BSCS（批共享通道块选择）通过保留批共享的通道子集来减少FFN计算，可执行紧凑密集FFN；3）CPGR（CGLA先验引导精化）解码器复用编码器端变化响应进行粗到细的预测。

Result: 在四个遥感变化检测基准上，AdaDINO取得了与VFM基线相当或更优的性能，在类别无关的SYSU-CD数据集上增益最大。在移除62.5%的FFN隐藏宽度后，AdaDINO在SYSU-CD上仍达到85.29%的F1分数，同时实现1.41倍吞吐量加速。

Conclusion: AdaDINO通过将冻结的DINO编码器与双时相交互机制在骨干网络内部进行适配，有效弥合了VFM单图像预训练与变化检测双时相推理需求之间的差距，实现了效率与性能的良好平衡。

Abstract: Vision foundation models (VFMs) such as DINO are pretrained for single-image representation, whereas remote sensing change detection requires reasoning over a bi-temporal pair. Existing VFM-based methods usually encode the two images independently and compare them only afterward, leaving the VFM backbone unaware of cross-temporal relations. To bridge this mismatch, we present AdaDINO, a pair-aware in-backbone adaptation framework that equips a frozen DINO encoder with bi-temporal interaction for efficient change detection. Its core component, Change-aware Gated Local Adaptation (CGLA), couples the two streams after selected frozen blocks and injects a shared temporal residual into them with opposite signs, enhancing genuine change responses while preserving the pair midpoint. Batch-Shared Chunk Selection (BSCS) further reduces feed-forward network (FFN) computation by retaining a batch-shared subset of channel chunks that can be executed as a compact dense FFN. A CGLA-Prior-Guided Refinement (CPGR) decoder reuses encoder-side change responses for coarse-to-fine prediction. Experiments on four remote sensing change detection benchmarks show that AdaDINO achieves competitive or superior performance against VFM-based baselines, with the largest gain on the category-agnostic SYSU-CD dataset. With 62.5% of the FFN hidden width removed, AdaDINO still achieves an F1 score of 85.29% on SYSU-CD while delivering a 1.41$\times$ throughput speedup. The code will be released.

</details>


### [62] [AgriField-40K: Adapting Vision Models to Agriculture With Efficient Continual Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07984)
*Vasileios Tzouras,Paraskevas Pegios,Lazaros Nalpantidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了 AgriField-40K，一个从17个公开数据源构建的农业场景数据集，并在此基础上提出 AgriMAE——一种参数高效的持续预训练方法，通过在自然图像预训练的掩码自编码器上仅训练轻量适配器，实现了与全量微调相当甚至更优的性能，同时可训练参数减少高达9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业场景的计算机视觉对于精准农业至关重要，但现有方法依赖昂贵的标注成本和大型预训练模型的高代价适配，缺乏高效的领域预训练方案。

Method: 构建覆盖多种作物、杂草和田间条件的 AgriField-40K 数据集；提出 AgriMAE 基线，采用在自然图像预训练的 MAE 上进行参数高效的持续预训练（仅训练轻量适配器），并探索语义特征重建作为替代预训练目标。

Result: AgriMAE 在多个下游任务上持续提升性能，能以最多9倍更少的可训练参数匹配甚至超越全量微调的效果。

Conclusion: AgriField-40K 是农业视觉领域持续预训练的实用资源，参数高效的适配策略为农业场景提供了低成本、高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Field-based agricultural computer vision is important for precision agriculture, yet it largely depends on expensive annotations and costly adaptation of large pretrained models. We introduce AgriField-40K, a field-centric dataset curated from 17 public resources and covering diverse crops, weeds, and field conditions. Building on this, we present AgriMAE, a parameter-efficient continual pretraining baseline that adapts a masked autoencoder pretrained on natural images by training only lightweight adapters. We further explore semantic feature reconstruction as an alternative pretraining objective and evaluate transfer across multiple tasks. AgriMAE consistently improves downstream performance and can match or even outperform full fine-tuning while using up to $9\times$ fewer trainable parameters, showing that AgriField-40K is a practical resource for continual pretraining in agricultural vision. Project page: https://dtu-pas.github.io/agrifield40k/

</details>


### [63] [Advantage-Guided Gate: Reshaping Open-Ended Reasoning for Vision-Based Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07987)
*Ling Lin,Yang Bai,Congcong Zhu,Jiangming Shi,Meng Wang,Yang Long,Jingrun Chen,Ling Shao,Huazhu Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个优势引导的门控框架（Advantage-Guided Gating Framework），通过将多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的逐步推理建模为有限时域决策过程，引入蒙特卡洛值评估提供中间监督信号，并设计Step-Advantage Gate和Trajectory-Advantage Gate分别动态选择高价值推理步骤和高质量完整推理轨迹，从而纠正推理过程中的偏差，提升MLLMs在视觉空间理解与推理任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs在复杂空间场景理解与推理任务中展现出潜力，但其开放式推理过程容易产生决策错误和误差累积，导致答案质量不稳定，因此需要一种动态干预和纠正推理偏差的机制。

Method: 将逐步推理建模为有限时域决策过程；在推理树上引入蒙特卡洛值评估提供中间监督信号；提出Step-Advantage Gate和Trajectory-Advantage Gate两个门控模块；训练时利用多分支采样生成的推理树进行门控监督学习，结合共享参数初始化与任务特定头实现跨任务鲁棒性；推理时贪心选择高值前缀推理步骤，并根据问题类型选择最优推理头；构建Reasoning-Tree-160k数据集并进行两阶段学习。

Result: 大量实验表明该优势引导门控框架能有效提升基准MLLMs在视觉空间理解与推理任务中的性能，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 优势引导的门控框架通过动态干预推理过程、选择高价值推理步骤和高质量轨迹，有效缓解了MLLMs推理误差累积的问题，显著增强了空间理解与推理任务的准确性和稳定性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated significant potential in complex spatial scene understanding and reasoning tasks. However, their open-ended reasoning process is prone to decision errors and error accumulation, leading to instability in answer quality. To address this, we propose an advantage-guided gating framework that dynamically intervenes in and corrects deviations during the reasoning process. Specifically, we model step-by-step reasoning as a finite-horizon decision process and introduce Monte Carlo value evaluation on the reasoning tree to provide intermediate supervision signals. The framework includes Step-Advantage Gate and Trajectory-Advantage Gate, which dynamically select high-value reasoning steps and high-quality complete reasoning trajectories, respectively. During training, we perform supervised learning for the gates using reasoning trees generated via multi-branch sampling, and combine shared-parameter initialization with task-specific heads to achieve cross-task robustness and diversity. During inference, the model greedily selects high-value prefix reasoning steps while choosing the optimal reasoning head based on the problem type, thereby significantly improving the accuracy of the final answer. Furthermore, we constructed the Reasoning-Tree-160k dataset and performed two-stage learning on it. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this advantage-guided gating framework effectively enhances the performance of benchmark MLLMs in visual-based spatial understanding and reasoning tasks. The code is open to the public for research: https://github.com/LingLin-ll/Advantage-Guided-Gate.

</details>


### [64] [MRBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Human Motion-Text Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07993)
*Fulong Liu,Liang Xu,Chengqun Yang,Yuhao Zhang,Yichao Yan,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MRBench，一个全面的运动-文本检索基准，包含3,390个来自多源（动作捕捉、野外视频、合成视频和生成模型）的运动数据、118个细粒度类别，以及10,170条多粒度描述。同时提出了一种轻量级的粒度感知检索模型，通过冻结标准标题对齐模型并结合LLM生成的伪监督，实现混合粒度检索性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动-文本检索基准存在三个主要问题：运动数据以同质室内运动为主、运动分布不均衡、文本描述过于简单且重复。这些问题阻碍了对跨领域和跨粒度对齐能力的可靠评估，因此需要一个更具挑战性和代表性的基准。

Method: （1）构建MRBench：通过多阶段数据策展流程，包括候选过滤与平衡、语义对齐验证、多粒度运动接地描述生成，构建了涵盖118个细粒度类别、来自四类来源的3,390个运动基准。（2）提出粒度感知模型：冻结标准标题对齐的检索模型，利用LLM生成的简洁和细粒度标题作为伪监督，训练额外的粒度特定运动提取器和文本适配器；推理时采用粒度感知分数融合，在保持分数可比性的前提下结合全局与适配相似度。

Result: 在MRBench上的广泛评估显示，现有检索基线存在显著的跨数据集泛化差距和对查询粒度的明显敏感性。提出的粒度感知模型在不牺牲标准标题性能的情况下，提升了混合粒度检索的效果。

Conclusion: MRBench为运动-语言对齐评估提供了一个全面的测试平台，有助于推动跨领域和跨粒度运动-文本检索研究的发展。

Abstract: Human motion-text retrieval provides a rigorous means of assessing cross-modal alignment. Prevailing benchmarks are dominated by homogeneous indoor motions, imbalanced motion distributions, and oversimplified, repetitive texts, which hinder the reliable measurement of cross-domain and cross-granularity alignment. We thus introduce MRBench, a comprehensive motion-text retrieval benchmark featuring heterogeneous motions, broad and balanced category coverage, and reliable, discriminative, multi-granular descriptions. MRBench is constructed through a meticulously designed multi-stage data curation pipeline, which filters and balances candidates, verifies unambiguous semantic alignment, and generates motion-grounded descriptions at multiple granularities. The resulting benchmark contains 3,390 motions drawn from motion capture, in-the-wild videos, synthetic videos, and motion generative models, covering 118 fine-grained categories. Each motion is paired with concise, standard, and fine-grained descriptions, yielding 10,170 captions. Extensive evaluations of representative retrieval baselines on MRBench reveal a substantial cross-dataset generalization gap and pronounced sensitivity to query granularity. We propose a lightweight granularity-aware model anchored at a frozen standard-caption-aligned retrieval model. LLM-based concise and fine-grained captions provide pseudo-supervision for extra-branch granularity-specific motion extractors and text adapters. For inference, granularity-aware score fusion integrates global and adapted similarities while strictly maintaining score comparability across all description levels. The resulting model improves mixed-granularity retrieval without compromising standard-caption performance. We believe that our MRBench provides a comprehensive testbed for advancing motion-language alignment evaluation.

</details>


### [65] [PE-Mamba: Bidirectional Selective Layer Aggregation for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07999)
*Kutub Uddin,Nusrat Tasnim,Khalid Malik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PE-Mamba，一种基于预训练PE-Core视觉Transformer和轻量级LoRA适配的AI生成图像检测框架，通过双向选择性聚合器（BSA）、Softmax加权聚合器（SWA）和Sigmoid门控融合（SGA）三个互补组件实现跨层特征聚合，在UniversalFakeDetect和AIGCDetect上分别达到96.6%/99.5%和95.3%/98.1%的mACC/mAP，超越了18个检测器，仅训练总参数的1.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型的快速发展使得AI生成图像与真实图像之间的差距日益缩小，检测难度不断加大。现有基于视觉Transformer的检测器通常采用加权求和策略聚合各层中间表示，忽略了层次特征从浅层纹理到深层语义的有序语义递进关系，这是一个重要的研究空白。

Method: PE-Mamba框架基于预训练的PE-Core视觉Transformer，采用轻量级LoRA适配，引入三个互补组件：1）双向选择性聚合器（BSA）通过前向和后向选择性扫描处理逐层分类token，前向扫描逐步累积从浅到深的取证证据，后向扫描进行从深到浅的上下文精炼；2）Softmax加权聚合器（SWA）计算所有层token的学习式全局摘要作为互补聚合路径；3）Sigmoid门控融合（SGA）通过可学习的标量门控自适应融合BSA和SWA的输出，动态平衡方向性序列证据与全局层间聚合。

Result: 在UniversalFakeDetect数据集上达到96.6% mACC和99.5% mAP，在AIGCDetect数据集上达到95.3% mACC和98.1% mAP，超越18个现有检测器，展现出优异的跨生成模型泛化能力，且仅训练总参数的1.3%（其中LoRA仅占0.13%）。

Conclusion: PE-Mamba通过引入双向顺序扫描来建模层次特征的语义递进关系，结合Softmax全局聚合和Sigmoid门控自适应融合，有效提升了AI生成图像检测的准确性和泛化能力，同时保持极高的参数效率，为该领域提供了一种新的跨层特征聚合范式。

Abstract: AI-generated image (AIGI) detection has become increasingly challenging due to the rapid advancement of generative models and the diminishing gap between synthetic and authentic content. Existing vision transformer-based detectors commonly rely on weighted-sum strategies to aggregate intermediate representations across transformer layers, often overlooking the inherently ordered semantic progression of hierarchical features from shallow texture cues to deep semantic representations. In this work, we propose \textbf{PE-Mamba}, a novel framework built upon a pre-trained PE-Core vision transformer with lightweight LoRA adaptation that introduces three complementary components for cross-layer feature aggregation and fusion. First, a bidirectional selective aggregator (BSA) processes layer-wise classification tokens through forward and backward selective scans, where the forward scan progressively accumulates shallow-to-deep forensic evidence, and the backward scan performs deep-to-shallow contextual refinement to reinterpret low-level cues in light of high-level semantic context. Second, a softmax-weighted aggregator (SWA) computes a learned global summary of all layer tokens as a complementary aggregation path. Third, a sigmoid-gated blend (SGA) adaptively fuses the BSA and SWA outputs via a learnable scalar gate, allowing the model to dynamically balance directional sequential evidence and global layer-wise aggregation. Extensive experiments on UniversalFakeDetect (96.6\% mACC, 99.5\% mAP) and AIGCDetect (95.3\% mACC, 98.1\% mAP) demonstrate that \methodname{} outperforms 18 detectors with superior generalization across diverse generative models, while training only 1.3\% of total parameters (0.13\% for LoRA alone).

</details>


### [66] [Evidence-Grounded Forensic Reasoning for Detecting and Grounding Multi-Modal Media Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08009)
*Yichun Yeh,Yiheng Li,Xiaobo Hu,Zhen Lei,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于证据锚定的取证推理（EFR）框架，用于多模态媒体操纵检测与定位（DGM4），通过锚定-验证推理链和模态解耦优势路由机制，实现可解释、可验证的伪造检测，并在实验中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DGM4方法产生黑盒检测结果，缺乏决策依据；直接应用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）面临两个难题：(1)模型生成的解释与预测证据位置脱节，导致归因不可验证；(2)证据-结论一致性的强化需要主动优化，但统一训练信号无法区分定位token和分类token，使多头联合训练不可靠。

Method: 提出EFR框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Anchor-and-Verify推理链：先进行模态隔离感知再进行跨模态比较，以结论坐标为显式锚点，强制下游证据在空间上对应；(2) 可验证奖励系统：在训练中强化证据-结论一致性；(3) Modality-Decoupled Advantage（MDA）路由机制：缓解跨预测任务间的信用分配错误。

Result: 实验表明EFR在DGM4任务上取得最先进（SOTA）性能，同时生成结构化取证推理记录，明确将解释与证据绑定，实现可验证的归因。

Conclusion: EFR框架有效解决了MLLMs在伪造检测中解释与证据脱节的问题，通过显式锚定机制和模态解耦路由实现了既准确又可解释的多模态媒体操纵检测。

Abstract: Fake news increasingly relies on cross-modal image-text forgeries, making transparent and verifiable reasoning chains an urgent need for Detecting and Grounding Multi-Modal Media Manipulation (DGM4). Existing methods produce black-box detection results without any decision rationale, limiting their reliability in forensic practice. Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) offer a natural path toward explainability, but applying them to DGM4 raises two difficulties. First, models tend to generate explanations disconnected from predicted evidence locations, producing unverified attribution. Second, enforcing evidence-conclusion consistency requires active optimization, yet uniform training signals fail to distinguish localization tokens from classification tokens, making multi-head joint training unreliable. We propose a multi-modal manipulation detector based on an Evidence-Grounded Forensic Reasoning (EFR) framework. EFR introduces an Anchor-and-Verify reasoning chain that enforces modality-isolated perception before cross-modal comparison, with conclusion coordinates as explicit anchors to which downstream evidence must spatially correspond. A verifiable reward system then enforces evidence-conclusion consistency during training, while a Modality-Decoupled Advantage (MDA) routing mechanism mitigats credit misassignment across prediction tasks. Experiments show that EFR achieves state-of-the-art performance while producing structured forensic reasoning records that explicitly bind explanations to evidence.

</details>


### [67] [EgoTrack3D: A Modular Framework for Egocentric 3D Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08016)
*Jan Kulik,Bjarni Dagur Thor Karason,Yung-Hsu Yang,Boyang Sun,Marc Pollefeys,Xi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoTrack3D是一个模块化框架，可直接从自我中心RGB视频中重建并维护动态3D场景表示，通过将2D分割掩码提升到全局3D坐标系，结合基于点的运动评分和基于体素的合并启发式来关联对象轨迹。在Aria Digital Twin数据集上相比最强基线PCL提升11%，并支持在稀疏深度等退化条件下保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自我中心视频理解3D场景对机器人和自主导航至关重要，但快速视角变化和部分遮挡使得构建结构化表示具有挑战性。现有3D跟踪和场景图构建方法主要处理显式交互或假设静态场景，无法捕捉复杂动态。

Method: 提出模块化框架EgoTrack3D：将2D分割掩码提升到全局3D坐标系，使用基于点的运动评分机制配合基于体素的合并启发式进行对象轨迹关联，并通过稀疏3D边界框估计和交互引导的动态关联增强对退化条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在Aria Digital Twin（ADT）数据集上相对于最强基线实现了11%的PCL（正确位置百分比）提升，同时处理了静态和动态对象的持久3D跟踪这一更通用的场景。

Conclusion: EgoTrack3D能够在自我中心视频中维持准确的动态3D场景表示，并且在用稀疏3D边界框估计替代密集深度图等真实部署约束下保持鲁棒性，为机器人感知和自主导航提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Understanding 3D scenes from egocentric video is fundamental for robotics and autonomous navigation, yet rapid viewpoint changes and partial occlusions make building structured representations challenging. Existing 3D tracking and scene graph construction methods primarily address explicit interactions or assume static scenes, limiting their ability to capture complex dynamics. We introduce EgoTrack3D, a modular framework that reconstructs and maintains a dynamic 3D scene representation directly from egocentric RGB video. The framework lifts 2D segmentation masks into a global 3D coordinate frame, using a point-based motion scoring mechanism alongside a voxel-based merging heuristic to associate object tracks. EgoTrack3D maintains accurate representations over time, achieving an 11% improvement in percentage of correct locations (PCL) relative to the strongest baseline on the Aria Digital Twin (ADT) dataset, while addressing the more general setting of persistent 3D tracking for both static and dynamic objects. Furthermore, to demonstrate the system's robustness under degraded conditions that simulate real-world deployment constraints, we replace dense depth maps with sparse 3D bounding box estimation and integrate interaction-guided dynamic association, enabling EgoTrack3D to maintain accurate spatial representations despite noisy observations.

</details>


### [68] [Evidence-RL: Towards Evidence-intensive Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08021)
*Haojie Huang,Xinlei Yu,Chengming Xu,Zhangquan Chen,Cheng Yang,Qingdong He,Yu Yang,Jiangning Zhang,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实证据解缠(CED)方法，一种用于VLM接地能力的训练时证据审计方法。CED通过中和对象中心的证据区域并比较支持度下降来判断答案是否因果依赖局部证据，结合GRPO训练奖励正确且依赖证据路径的答案。方法无需问题级证据标注，无推理开销，在9个基准和4个骨干网络上优于现有RL后训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言模型(VLM)往往依赖语言先验、数据集捷径或无关视觉上下文来回答问题，而非真正基于图像中的具体证据。已有的感知后训练方法通过全局扰动或注意力代理鼓励使用图像，但未能检验采样答案是否因果地依赖于支撑它的局部证据。

Method: CED包含三个关键要素：1)对每个响应中和对象中心的证据区域，并与匹配的非证据区域对比支持度下降幅度；2)将该信号与答案正确性结合到GRPO中，奖励依赖证据路径而非捷径或干扰路径的正确答案；3)使用弱对象级提议，无需问题特定证据标注，且无推理开销。

Result: 在九个公开基准和四个骨干网络上，CED优于现有的基于强化学习的后训练方法，目标分析验证了其对象中心信号的有效性。

Conclusion: CED通过反事实证据审计有效地增强了VLM的接地能力，证明了将答案正确性与因果证据依赖相结合的训练信号能够引导模型更可靠地基于图像证据推理。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) should answer from concrete image evidence rather than language priors, dataset shortcuts, or irrelevant visual context. Existing perception-aware post-training methods encourage image use through global perturbations or attention proxies, but they do not test whether a sampled answer causally depends on the local evidence that supports it. We propose Counterfactual Evidence Disentanglement (CED), a training-time evidence audit for VLM grounding. For each response, CED neutralizes an object-centric Evidence Region and compares the resulting support drop against matched non-evidence Regions. We combine this signal with answer correctness inside GRPO, rewarding correct answers that rely on the evidence path rather than shortcut or nuisance paths. CED uses weak object-level proposals, requires no question-specific evidence annotations, and adds no inference-time overhead. Across nine public benchmarks and four backbones, CED outperforms prior RL-based post-training methods, with targeted analyses verifying its object-centric signal.

</details>


### [69] [ZOMP: Zeroth-Order Multi-Modal Prompt Tuning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08060)
*Sajjad Ghiasvand,Yifan Yang,Mahnoosh Alizadeh,Ramtin Pedarsani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ZOMP方法，这是一种完全前向传播的零阶多模态提示调优方法，通过在冻结的CLIP模型的视觉和文本分支中同时调优深层提示，利用交叉模态低秩重参数化、梯度校正动量和预算索引的秩调度，在5,000次查询预算下显著优于现有的无反向传播提示调优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调视觉语言模型（如CLIP）通常需要完整的反向传播，但在内存受限的边缘设备和专有模型部署场景中，只有前向传播可用。现有的无反向传播零阶提示调优方法要么只在单一模态中调优提示，要么搜索空间过大导致收敛需要数千次前向传播，在现实查询预算下不实用。

Method: ZOMP由三个核心组件构成：（1）交叉模态低秩重参数化，通过共享因子将视觉和文本两个分支绑定，保持有效搜索维度较小；（2）梯度校正动量项，稳定噪声化的零阶估计；（3）预算索引的秩调度，随着查询预算消耗逐步解锁模型容量。该方法基于同步扰动随机逼近（SPSA）实现。

Result: 在13个视觉语言基准上，使用匹配的5,000次查询预算，ZOMP在少样本准确率和查询效率方面均一致优于现有的无反向传播提示调优方法，并且在base-to-new、跨数据集迁移和分布外设置中具有更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 联合利用多模态性和低秩结构是实现实用、查询高效的无反向传播提示调优的有效途径，为边缘设备和专有模型场景下的高效模型适配提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning vision-language models such as CLIP typically requires backpropagation (BP) through the full model, which is infeasible when only forward-pass access is available, as is common for memory-constrained edge devices and proprietary model deployments. Prior BP-free, zeroth-order prompt-tuning methods avoid this requirement but often tune prompts in a single modality or optimize over a search space large enough that convergence requires thousands of forward passes, which is impractical under realistic query budgets. We propose ZOMP (Zeroth-Order Multimodal Prompt tuning), a query-efficient, fully forward-only method that tunes deep prompts in both the vision and text branches of a frozen CLIP model using simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation. ZOMP combines three ingredients: a cross-modal low-rank reparameterization that ties the two branches through a shared factor and keeps the effective search dimensionality small, a gradient-correction momentum term that stabilizes the noisy zeroth-order estimate, and a budget-indexed rank schedule that unlocks capacity as the query budget is spent. Across 13 vision-language benchmarks under a matched 5,000-query budget, ZOMP consistently outperforms prior BP-free prompt-tuning methods in both few-shot accuracy and query efficiency, and it generalizes better across base-to-new, cross-dataset transfer, and out-of-distribution settings. Our results show that jointly exploiting multimodality and low-rank structure is an effective route to practical, query-efficient BP-free prompt tuning.

</details>


### [70] [EvBS: Event-guided Blur Synthesis for Domain-adaptive Motion Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08066)
*Junsik Jung,Seokryun Choi,Yoonki Cho,Woo Jae Kim,Andrew Jeong,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EvBS，一个基于事件相机引导的模糊合成框架，通过解耦运动与视觉内容，分别利用内在运动和外在运动生成多样化的训练对，用于微调预训练去模糊模型以适应目标域，在多个基准上显著提升了跨域去模糊性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练去模糊模型在真实场景中常因训练与测试分布间的域偏移而性能下降，现有方法受限于运动与视觉内容的内在耦合，难以有效生成多样化的训练数据以适配目标域。

Method: 利用事件相机的高时间分辨率将运动与视觉内容解耦，提出两种互补策略：内在模糊合成（用自身运动模式模糊清晰内容）和外在模糊合成（将模糊块的运动迁移到不同清晰内容上），从而生成打破自然耦合限制的多样化训练对，通过微调实现域自适应。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，EvBS能有效增强现有去模糊模型在未见测试数据集上的鲁棒性，显著提升域自适应去模糊性能。

Conclusion: EvBS通过事件相机辅助的运动-内容解耦，实现了生成多样化的合成模糊训练对，为预训练去模糊模型的域自适应提供了一种有效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Motion deblurring has achieved remarkable progress with deep learning, yet pre-trained deblurring models often suffer from performance degradation in real-world scenarios due to the domain shift between training and testing distributions. To remedy this, we propose EvBS, an event-guided blur synthesis framework that generates diverse training pairs for calibrating pre-trained models to the target domain. While existing methods are constrained by the inherent entanglement between motion and visual content, our method leverages the high temporal resolution of event cameras to effectively decouple them. This enables us to utilize not only the intrinsic motion that is inherent to the given content but also extrinsic motion transferred from different sources within the target domain, thereby facilitating effective adaptation via fine-tuning. Specifically, EvBS comprises two complementary strategies: Intrinsic-Blur Synthesis, which blurs sharp contents with their own motion patterns, and Extrinsic-Blur Synthesis, which transfers motion from blurry patches to distinct sharp content. This approach generates a diverse set of training pairs that break the inherent constraints of naturally coupled motion and content, resulting in enhanced domain-adaptive deblurring performance. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that EvBS effectively enhances the robustness of existing deblurring models on unseen testing datasets.

</details>


### [71] [NeuroGuard: Neural Gradient Update Aware of Representation Damage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08068)
*Taigo Sakai,Kazuhito Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuroGuard是一种无需增加可学习参数的LT-CIL更新控制方法，通过自适应梯度缩放、置信度排序的知识蒸馏重加权和脆弱性混合熵门控三个组件，在五个LT-CIL设置中全面优于DGR基线，并在四个主要基准对比中取得最佳任务无关准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LT-CIL方法主要关注重放、分类器或损失函数的设计，而忽略了特征表示在任务边界处更新强度这一关键因素。作者希望探索如何更好地控制特征表示的更新程度，以在长尾类别增量学习中兼顾新类学习与旧类保持。

Method: NeuroGuard包含三个组件：1) 自适应梯度缩放(AGS)将教师不确定性转化为任务级梯度缩放因子；2) 置信度排序知识蒸馏重加权(CRK)对教师预测不够果断的重放样本赋予更大的知识蒸馏权重；3) 脆弱性混合熵门控(FBE)将旧记忆泄漏信息纳入缩放决策。该方法直接作用于DGR基线，不添加任何可学习参数。

Result: 在五个LT-CIL设置中，NeuroGuard均优于DGR基线；在四个主要基准对比中获得最佳任务无关准确率。新旧类别的准确率均有提升，中频类别的准确率在全部五个设置中一致提升。对照实验表明效果非来自通用梯度抑制，AGS在全部五个设置中均优于匹配的固定缩放控制。

Conclusion: 任务边界特定的特征更新缩放比在整个学习过程中应用统一平均缩放更有效，证明了更新控制策略在LT-CIL中的重要性。

Abstract: Long-tailed class-incremental learning (LT-CIL) must learn new classes from imbalanced streams while retaining old classes. Existing methods mainly change replay, classifiers, or losses. We study a different factor, namely how strongly the feature representation should be updated at each task boundary. We propose NeuroGuard, an update-control method added to DGR, a replay-based LT-CIL baseline, without adding learnable parameters. NeuroGuard preserves DGR's replay memory, classifier, and set of loss terms. Adaptive Gradient Scaling (AGS) converts teacher uncertainty into one task-wise gradient scale. Confidence-Ranked Knowledge Distillation Reweighting (CRK) gives larger knowledge-distillation weights to replay samples that the teacher predicts less decisively. Fragility-Blended Entropy Gate (FBE) adds old-memory leakage to the scale decision. Across five LT-CIL settings, NeuroGuard improves over DGR in every setting. In the four main benchmark comparisons, it achieves the best task-agnostic accuracy among the compared methods. The gains extend to both old- and new-class accuracy, while medium-frequency accuracy improves consistently across all five settings. Controlled comparisons show that the gain does not come from generic gradient suppression: AGS outperforms a matched fixed-scale control in all five settings, demonstrating that boundary-specific scaling is more effective than applying the same average scale throughout learning.

</details>


### [72] [SCTD 3.0: Sonar Common Target Detection in the Wild - A Large-Scale, Multi-Scene Dataset from Real Marine Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08106)
*Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCTD 3.0，一个大规模真实测量的合成孔径声纳（SAS）图像数据集，包含超过10,000张高质量图像片段，覆盖10类典型水下目标、多种海床地形、观测角度和频率波段，并构建了多任务基准（目标检测、细粒度分类、属性预测）以评估深度学习模型的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAS目标识别数据集规模小且局限于单一场景，无法反映真实检测中复杂的声散射、多样的海底地形和多姿态成像，制约了数据驱动的目标识别方法的发展。需要一个大规模、真实的基准数据集来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了包含超过10,000张多频率（240 kHz、450 kHz等）真实SAS图像片段的数据集，覆盖10类目标、多种海底地貌、观测角度和频率波段；设计了严格的层次化标注协议，解耦物理属性、部署特征和散射现象（材料、几何、内部结构、埋没状态、阴影完整性、镜面高光、边缘衍射、共振效应）的标注；构建了目标检测、细粒度分类和属性预测的多任务基准。

Result: 数据集涵盖多频率、多场景、多视角的丰富真实SAS图像，并基于该数据集评估了主流深度学习模型在跨域、跨场景、跨频率、跨视角泛化设置下的性能。

Conclusion: SCTD 3.0为开放水域环境下鲁棒的水下目标感知提供了关键的数据基石，有望推动数据驱动的SAS目标识别研究。

Abstract: Synthetic Aperture Sonar (SAS) is core for wide-area detection of small underwater targets. However, large-scale, high-quality SAS datasets are scarce, hindering data-driven recognition. Existing benchmarks are small and limited to single scenarios, failing to reproduce complex acoustic scattering, diverse seabeds, and multi-pose imaging in real detection. To fill this gap, we introduce SCTD 3.0 - a large-scale real-measured dataset for Sonar Common Target Detection in the Wild in natural waters. It contains over 10,000 high-quality real SAS image snippets from multi-frequency systems (240 kHz, 450 kHz, and others), covering ten typical target categories across varied seabed geomorphologies, with multiple observation angles, detection ranges, and frequency bands. We establish a rigorous hierarchical annotation protocol that decouples labeling of intrinsic physical properties, deployment characteristics, and scattering phenomena - covering material, geometry, internal structure, burial state, shadow integrity, specular highlights, edge diffraction, and resonance effects. This enables fine-grained target characterization. We also construct a multi-task benchmark for object detection, fine-grained classification, and attribute prediction, evaluating mainstream deep learning models under cross-domain, cross-scene, cross-frequency, and cross-view generalization. SCTD 3.0 is expected to provide a critical data cornerstone for robust underwater target perception in open-water environments. SCTD 3.0 is available at https://github.com/automlresearch/SCTD-3.0.

</details>


### [73] [SUMI: Scalable Unified Model for 3D Point Cloud Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08115)
*Yanlong LI,Kanchana Thilakarathna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SUMI是一个用于点云补全的扩散增强细化模块，通过将噪声几何特征注入与粗结构特征的交叉注意力中，在保持全局一致性的同时细化局部几何细节，可灵活集成到现有粗到细模型中，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有粗到细点云补全方法虽然改善了全局结构恢复，但细化阶段往往受限于简单的上采样操作和与粗结构特征交互不足，导致局部细节重建困难。

Method: 提出SUMI扩散增强细化模块：不同于以往将扩散作为独立点生成器的做法，SUMI将噪声几何特征注入到与粗结构特征的交叉注意力中，利用逆向去噪过程细化局部几何，同时保持全局一致性；该模块可灵活集成到现有粗到细模型中。

Result: 在PCN、ShapeNet-55/34和MVP数据集上均优于强基线：PCN上取得最佳整体CD和F1分数，ShapeNet-55上CD最多降低16.1%，MVP上在所有输出密度下均取得最佳CD。

Conclusion: SUMI作为一种灵活的扩散增强细化模块，有效解决了粗到细点云补全中局部细节重建的瓶颈问题，验证了将扩散模型嵌入细化阶段而非独立使用的有效性。

Abstract: Point cloud completion commonly follows a coarse-to-fine paradigm, where a low-density coarse shape is first predicted and then upsampled to the target resolution. Although recent methods have improved global structure recovery, the fine stage often remains limited by simple upsampling and insufficient interaction with coarse structural features, making local detail reconstruction challenging. We propose SUMI, a diffusion-enhanced refinement module for coarse-to-fine point cloud completion. Unlike prior diffusion-based completion methods that use diffusion as a standalone point generator, SUMI injects noisy geometric features into cross-attention with coarse structural features, enabling reverse denoising to refine local geometry while preserving global consistency. SUMI can also be integrated into existing coarse-to-fine models as a flexible refinement module. Experiments on PCN, ShapeNet-55/34, and MVP demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines. SUMI achieves the best overall CD and F1-score on PCN, reduces CD by up to 16.1% on ShapeNet-55, and obtains the best CD across all output densities on MVP.

</details>


### [74] [Staying True to the Origin: Continuous Image Stylization with Smooth Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08125)
*Rui Xu,Hanmo Zhang,Songhua Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散Transformer（DiT）的连续可控图像风格化方法，通过两阶段训练策略和风格强度感知样条插值，实现对风格化强度的精确连续控制，同时保持内容图像的语义结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在文本和图像条件编辑上表现出色，但要在保持内容图像语义的同时参考另一图像的风格模式仍具挑战，容易产生不可控的风格化结果。作者希望从连续控制的角度解决此问题，实现内容保留与风格化效果之间的平滑过渡。

Method: 提出两阶段训练策略结合风格强度感知样条公式：第一阶段训练模型产生强风格化输出并尽可能保持内容语义；第二阶段冻结基础模型，学习一组锚点投影器将不同风格化强度映射到模型参数空间。推理时在低秩空间执行风格强度感知样条插值，实现对风格化强度的连续控制，尽管模型仅用少量离散强度级别训练。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法支持对风格化强度的精确连续操控，并能与现代DiT模型生成高保真结果。

Conclusion: 通过低秩空间中的样条插值，即使在仅用少量离散强度级别训练的情况下，也能实现连续可控的图像风格化，为基于DiT的多参考编辑模型提供了新的连续性控制方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative models have achieved remarkable performance in text- and image-conditioned editing. However, preserving the content of a given image while referencing style patterns from another remains challenging, often leading to uncontrollable stylization results. In this paper, we approach image stylization from the perspective of continuous control, aiming to enable modern Diffusion Transformer (DiT)-based multi-reference editing models to (1) faithfully preserve the semantic structure of the content image, (2) render strong stylization effects, and (3) smoothly transition between the two. To this end, we propose a simple yet effective two-stage training strategy along with a style-strength-aware spline formulation. Specifically, in the first stage, the model is trained to produce strongly stylized outputs while preserving the content semantics as much as possible. In the second stage, with the base model frozen, we learn a set of anchor projectors that map various stylization strengths into the model parameter space. During inference, by performing style-strength-aware spline interpolation in a low-rank space, our method enables continuous control over stylization strength, even though the model is trained with only a few discrete strength levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method supports precise and continuous manipulation of stylization strength while generating high-fidelity results with modern DiT models. Project page: https://reychiaro.github.io/StyleController.

</details>


### [75] [When Does An Extra View Help? Adapting Single-View 3D Reconstruction with Extra Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08132)
*Y Huynh,Duc Thanh Nguyen,Thao Minh Le,Mohamed Abdelrazek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ASV3D框架，通过一张额外辅助图像来提升单视图3D重建的质量，包含零样本适配和基于对比学习的优化适配两种策略，在基准数据集和真实数据集上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像3D重建的关键挑战在于缺少从其他视角获取的关键信息来完成3D结构。虽然使用额外视图可能有助于解决问题，但现有方法缺乏将额外视图整合进单视图3D重建原理的机制。

Method: 提出ASV3D框架，包含两种适配策略：(i)零样本适配方案，无需重新训练即可利用辅助图像改善重建质量；(ii)优化适配方案，通过对比学习进一步增强视觉保真度和跨视图一致性。

Result: 将该框架应用于两个最先进的单视图3D重建流程，在基准数据集和真实世界数据集上均稳定提升了重建精度和鲁棒性，在定量指标和人类偏好方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: ASV3D有效解决了单视图3D重建中额外视图整合的难题，为无约束多视角输入下的3D重建提供了可靠的改进方案，并开源了代码和真实世界物体数据集以供后续研究。

Abstract: Reconstruction of 3D objects from a single image is a challenging research problem in computer vision. The key challenge is the lack of critical information from viewpoints to complete 3D structures. Using an additional view may help to resolve the issue. However, there is no mechanism that can integrate the extra view into the single-view 3D reconstruction principle. We address this challenge by proposing ASV3D, a framework for adapting single-view 3D object reconstruction to test-time data with support from one additional image. We introduce two adaptation strategies: (i) a zero-shot adaptation scheme that leverages the auxiliary image to improve the reconstruction quality of an object without retraining, and (ii) an optimised adaptation scheme that further enhances visual fidelity and cross-view consistency via contrastive learning. We apply our ASV3D to improve two state-of-the-art single-view 3D reconstruction pipelines on both benchmark and real-world datasets. Results demonstrate that our approach consistently improves reconstruction accuracy and robustness under unconstrained multi-view inputs, outperforming the baselines in both quantitative metrics and human preference. We publish our code and the real-world object dataset in our project page at https://github.com/YNhuHuynh/ASV3D/tree/main.

</details>


### [76] [Compositional Cross-Modality Translation via Whole-Volume Multitask Latent Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08135)
*Daniele Molino,Alessio Zoboli,Camillo Maria Caruso,Valerio Guarrasi,Paolo Soda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨模态医学图像翻译方法，通过大型3D变分自编码器提供体积先验，将翻译问题简化为条件流匹配问题，实现了整卷处理和多任务联合训练，支持零样本泛化和组合式跨数据集翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态医学图像翻译方法受限于两个耦合问题：仅处理2D切片或3D补丁而非完整体积，以及为每个翻译任务单独训练模型。这两个问题的根源在于缺乏足够强的体积先验，迫使生成模型同时学习解剖外观和跨模态映射，这在配对数据的规模下是病态问题。

Method: 该方法将目标解耦为两部分：利用大规模预训练的3D变分自编码器提供体积外观的紧凑潜在表示，将翻译简化为条件流匹配问题。通过压缩使得整卷处理成为可行，并采用分辨率感知的采样策略保持原生解剖尺度。在三个多中心数据集上，对跨模态（MRI→CT, CBCT→CT）和模态内（MRI→MRI）任务进行单一模型的联合训练。

Result: 在所有任务中，整卷处理优于补丁处理；多任务模型匹配任务特定基线的性能，同时用单一网络替代N个网络。联合训练实现了任务特定方法无法企及的能力：对训练中未见解剖区域的零样本泛化（SSIM在完全监督模型的0.15以内），以及沿未经直接监督的路径进行组合式跨数据集翻译。

Conclusion: 将强体积先验与多任务训练相结合，是走向超越训练分布泛化的合成系统的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Cross-modality medical image translation can reduce the burden of multi-modal acquisitions, yet the field remains constrained by two coupled limitations: methods operate on 2D slices or 3D patches rather than whole volumes, and train a separate model for each translation task. Both stem from a single cause, the absence of a sufficiently strong volumetric prior, which forces generative models to learn anatomical appearance and cross-modality mapping simultaneously, an ill-posed problem at the scale of available paired datasets. We propose to decouple these objectives. A large-scale pretrained 3D variational autoencoder provides a compact latent representation of volumetric appearance, reducing translation to a conditional flow-matching problem. This compression makes whole-volume processing tractable, while a resolution-aware sampling strategy preserves native anatomical scale. We train a single model jointly across inter-modality (MRI$\to$CT, CBCT$\to$CT) and intra-modality (MRI$\to$MRI) tasks over three multi-center datasets. Across all tasks, whole-volume processing outperforms its patch-based counterpart, and the multi-task model matches task-specific baselines while replacing $N$ networks with one. Crucially, joint training unlocks capabilities inaccessible to task-specific approaches: zero-shot generalization to anatomical regions unseen during training, within 0.15 SSIM of the fully supervised model, and compositional cross-dataset translation along paths never directly supervised. These results suggest that combining a strong volumetric prior with multitask training is a scalable route toward synthesis systems that generalize beyond their training distribution. Code is available at https://github.com/arco-group/Whole-Volume-Latent-FM.

</details>


### [77] [EFFEKT: Efficient Federated Knowledge Transfer to Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08138)
*Matteo Caligiuri,Francesco Barbato,Pietro Zanuttigh,Francesco Restuccia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EFFEKT框架，通过轻量级客户端代理模型与服务端基础模型协作，实现资源受限场景下的多域联邦学习，无需共享私有数据即可高效训练领域特定LoRA适配器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据保护法规推动了联邦学习的广泛应用，但不断增大的模型规模给客户端设备带来巨大计算负担，限制了FL在资源受限场景中的适用性。

Method: 提出EFFEKT框架，采用轻量级客户端代理模型与服务端基础模型(BM)协作的方式，通过新颖的双向交叉蒸馏策略维持特征空间对齐，在服务端高效训练领域特定LoRA适配器。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集和低功耗边缘设备上的实验表明，EFFEKT在大多数考虑领域中优于现有最先进基线方法，同时保持客户端侧轻量级计算。

Conclusion: EFFEKT证明了通过客户端代理模型与服务端基础模型协作的架构，可以在不牺牲隐私保护的前提下实现高效的多域联邦学习，且适用于资源受限的边缘设备场景。

Abstract: Recent data protection laws have accelerated the adoption of Federated Learning (FL) for privacy-preserving decentralized training. Nevertheless, increasing model sizes impose substantial computational demands on client devices, limiting FL applicability in resource-constrained settings. We introduce a novel multi-domain federated learning framework in which lightweight client-side proxy models collaborate with a server-side Foundation Model (FM) to learn new concepts without sharing private data. Our approach, EFFEKT, enables efficient server-side training of domain-specific LoRA adapters while preserving feature-space alignment between the FM and proxy extractors via novel bi-directional cross-distillation strategies. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets and deployments on low-power edge devices demonstrate improvements over state-of-the-art baselines in most considered domains while maintaining lightweight computation at the client side.

</details>


### [78] [Learning Structural Illumination for Unsupervised Low-light Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08153)
*Tianle Du,Peiyuan He,Hainuo Wang,Tianxiu Yu,Xiaojie Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RISE框架用于无监督低光图像增强，通过将相对光照结构与绝对曝光水平解耦，并从可靠亮区估计光照，同时引入双测光曝光参考实现场景自适应增强，在基准和真实场景中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督低光图像增强方法直接从整个低光输入估计照明，未分离空间变化的相对光照结构与绝对曝光水平，且无法防止低信噪比区域的偏差影响；同时固定曝光目标缺乏场景适应性，限制了对多样化光照条件的泛化能力。

Method: RISE框架包含两部分：一是相对光照结构估计，将相对光照结构与绝对曝光解耦，仅从可靠亮区推断光照，实现可解释且鲁棒的增强；二是双测光曝光参考机制，根据每张输入图像生成场景自适应的曝光目标，动态调整增强强度。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和真实世界泛化实验中，RISE在无监督低光图像增强方法中达到了最先进性能，并产生了视觉上自然的增强效果。

Conclusion: 通过解耦光照结构与曝光水平并引入场景自适应的曝光参考，RISE成功解决了现有无监督LLIE方法的局限，在增强质量和泛化能力上都实现了显著提升。

Abstract: Existing unsupervised low-light image enhancement (LLIE) methods often estimate illumination directly from the entire low-light input, without separating its spatially varying illumination pattern, termed relative illumination structure, from the absolute exposure level or preventing unreliable low signal-to-noise ratio regions from biasing the estimate. Moreover, fixed exposure targets impose a scene-agnostic enhancement criterion, limiting adaptation across diverse lighting conditions. Inspired by the spatial propagation of light, we propose a Relative Illumination Structure Estimation (RISE) framework that decouples relative illumination structure from absolute exposure and infers it from reliable bright regions, enabling interpretable and robust enhancement. For scene-adaptive exposure adjustment, we further propose a Dual-Metering Exposure Reference derived from each input, allowing RISE to adapt the enhancement strength to individual scenes and generalize across diverse lighting conditions. Extensive benchmark and real-world generalization experiments show that RISE achieves state-of-the-art performance among unsupervised LLIE methods while producing visually natural results.

</details>


### [79] [Wiener Representation Filtering for VLM Hallucination Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08167)
*Ameen Ali,Tamim Zoabi,Lidor Brami,Lior Wolf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的、事后（post-hoc）表示编辑技术，通过在语言骨干网络的表示空间中建模隐状态为真实与幻觉成分的叠加，利用Wiener型滤波器对深层前馈输出投影进行模式级衰减，从而在推理时不起改变模型结构的情况下减少视觉语言模型的目标幻觉。在LLaVA-1.5、MiniGPT-4、Gemma3和mPLUG-Owl2等多个模型上验证了有效性，并推广到视频理解和离散扩散语言模型场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在开放式描述和视觉问答中表现优异，但经常生成图片中不存在的对象、属性或关系，即"目标幻觉"。现有方法或需训练/微调，或在推理时牺牲速度，因此需要一种训练免费、不影响推理效率的幻觉消除方案。

Method: 该方法在语言骨干的表示空间中进行事后编辑。具体而言：(1) 使用小型配对数据集进行一次性离线校准，仅通过前向传播和二阶统计量估计所需的协方差结构，无梯度更新；(2) 将隐状态建模为真实成分与幻觉相关成分的叠加，推导Wiener型估计器，其最优增益由配对真实/幻觉表示的协方差以闭式给出；(3) 通过特征分解获得模式级衰减，并满足稳定性准则（滤波器对估计噪声连续响应）；(4) 修正一次性应用到选定深层的前馈输出投影上，推理时模型不变、速度不变。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5、MiniGPT-4、Gemma3和mPLUG-Owl2上，该方法在CHAIR、POPE和MME基准上持续降低目标幻觉，同时保持描述的流畅性和整体回答质量。此外，在TempCompass视频理解基准和用于接地对话的离散扩散语言模型上也验证了泛化性，表明表示过滤在时间视频推理和序列级去噪设定中同样能减少幻觉。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种高效、通用的幻觉缓解方案：不训练、不微调、推理开销为零，通过将校正吸收到现有权重中实现"一次校准、处处受益"的效果，并且不仅适用于静态图像VLM，也适用于视频理解和扩散语言模型等更广泛的场景。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) excel at open-ended captioning and visual QA but often describe objects, attributes, or relations absent from the image, a phenomenon known as object hallucination. We propose a {training-free, post-hoc representation editing technique} that operates in the representation space of the language backbone. The method performs a lightweight, one-time offline calibration on a modest paired dataset to estimate the required covariance structures, using only forward passes and empirical second-order statistics with no gradient updates or fine-tuning, after which the correction is absorbed directly into the model's existing weights. By modeling hidden states as a superposition of truthful and hallucination-associated components, we derive a Wiener-type estimator whose optimal gains are given in closed form from the covariances of paired truthful and hallucinated representations. An eigendecomposition yields mode-wise attenuation that respects a stability criterion, i.e., the filter responds continuously to estimation noise. The correction is applied once to the feed-forward output projections of selected deeper layers, at inference time, the model runs unchanged and at the same speed. Experiments on LLaVA-1.5, MiniGPT-4, Gemma3, and mPLUG-Owl2 demonstrate consistent reductions in object hallucination on CHAIR, POPE, and MME while maintaining caption fluency and overall response quality. We further demonstrate the generality of our approach on the TempCompass video understanding benchmark and on discrete diffusion language models for grounded dialogue, showing that representation filtering reduces hallucinations even in temporal video reasoning and multi-step, sequence-wide denoising settings.

</details>


### [80] [BAP-MOS: Bandit-Based Adaptive Prompting for Boundary-Sensitive Multi-Organ Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08191)
*Satvik Praveen,Shengji Jin,Ahmed Lamidi,Xin Qian,Yi Sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BAP-MOS，一种基于边界自适应提示的闭环框架，用于多器官超声分割。通过将提示选择建模为多臂老虎机问题（外层TPE优化参数、内层UCB-Tuned自适应调整），结合器官缩放负提示环，仅更新掩码解码器。在前列腺TRUS和外部PFUS1数据集上超越多种基线，显著减少边界误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多器官超声分割中，解剖学上相邻的结构需要联合描绘时，即使Dice分数很高，局部边界误差仍可能持续存在，传统方法难以解决这一挑战。

Method: 1) 将提示选择形式化为器官特定的多臂老虎机问题，支持框、点和组合提示；2) 外层TPE循环选择提示选择参数向量，内层UCB-Tuned循环基于有界的Dice-MSD-HD95验证探针奖励自适应调整各器官提示偏好；3) 引入器官缩放负提示环适配稀疏提示几何；4) 冻结图像和提示编码器，仅更新掩码解码器。

Result: 在合并的前列腺TRUS队列上达到Dice 0.982、HD95 0.482、MSD 0.204，相比最强常规基线HD95降低约48%、MSD降低45%；在外部PFUS1盆底超声语料库上的泛化测试结果良好。

Conclusion: 自适应提示分配是改善边界敏感的多器官超声分割的有效机制，无需修改基础模型主干即可实现显著性能提升。

Abstract: Multi-organ ultrasound segmentation remains challenging when anatomically adjacent structures must be delineated jointly, as localized boundary errors can persist even when Dice scores are high. To address these challenges, we propose Boundary-Adaptive Prompting for Multi-Organ Segmentation (BAP-MOS), a closed-loop adaptive prompting framework. BAP-MOS formulates prompt selection as an organ-specific multi-armed bandit problem over box, point, and combined prompts. An outer Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) loop selects the prompt-selection parameter vector, while an inner UCB-Tuned loop adapts per-organ prompt preferences during fine-tuning using a bounded Dice--MSD--HD95 validation-probe reward. The framework further introduces an organ-scaled negative prompt ring to adapt sparse prompt geometry across anatomical scales, while keeping the image and prompt encoders frozen and updating only the mask decoder. We evaluate BAP-MOS on pooled prostate-region TRUS cohorts against U-Net, nnU-Net, MedSAM, fixed-prompt SAM/MedSAM, and adaptive policy variants. On this benchmark, BAP-MOS achieves Dice 0.982, HD95 0.482, and MSD 0.204, reducing HD95 by approximately 48% and MSD by 45% relative to the strongest conventional baseline. To verify the generalization ability of the framework, we tested it on the external PFUS1 pelvic-floor ultrasound corpus using MedSAM and its adaptive strategy variants, and the results were good. These results support adaptive prompt allocation as an effective mechanism for improving boundary-sensitive multi-organ ultrasound segmentation without modifying the foundation-model backbone. Source Code is available at: https://github.com/SatvikPraveen/BAP-MOS

</details>


### [81] [VTO: Visual Tool Orchestration for Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08219)
*Rui Wang,Yeteng Wu,Xianling Zhang,Mengshi Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VTO，一个基于过程监督强化学习的视频异常检测框架，通过认知评估器和工具编排实现多步推理优化，在VAD-Tool基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习方法在视频异常检测中泛化能力差；多模态智能体虽有潜力但现有监督微调难以应对复杂编排，标准强化学习因粗粒度结果奖励导致过早终止。

Method: 提出VTO框架：(1)采用过程监督强化学习；(2)引入基础模型驱动的认知评估器提供上下文语义反馈；(3)通过过程监督认知对齐实现细粒度逐步监督；(4)显式惩罚逻辑截断并奖励完整因果链，优化多步推理的工具编排策略；(5)构建VAD-Tool层次化视觉工具集（12个专业工具）及对应基准。

Result: 在VAD-Tool基准上，VTO显著优于基线方法，工具调度准确率绝对提升最高达10.2%。

Conclusion: 过程监督强化学习结合认知评估器能有效改善多模态智能体在视频异常检测中的工具编排能力，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) is a critical yet challenging task due to the complex and diverse nature of real-world scenarios. Traditional deep learning approaches are fundamentally limited by poor generalization across diverse scenarios. While multimodal agents offer a promising tool-learning paradigm for VAD, current systems relying on supervised fine-tuning struggle with complex orchestration, and standard reinforcement learning often causes premature termination due to coarse-grained outcome rewards. To address these challenges, we propose VTO, a process-supervised reinforcement learning framework. Moving beyond static tool usage, VTO enables the agent to dynamically explore and interact with the environment. Specifically, we introduce a foundation model-driven cognitive evaluator to provide context-aware semantic feedback, which is seamlessly integrated into a Process-Supervised Cognitive Alignment that delivers fine-grained, step-wise supervision. By explicitly penalizing logical truncation and rewarding complete causal chains, the agent optimizes its multi-step reasoning policy for interrelated tool orchestration. To support our proposed framework, we meticulously crafted VAD-Tool, a hierarchical visual tool set comprising 12 specialized vision tools spanning from entity tracking to high-stakes hazard detection, and established the corresponding benchmark for rigorous multi-step reasoning evaluation. Extensive experiments on VAD-Tool demonstrate that VTO significantly outperforms baselines, achieving up to a 10.2\% absolute accuracy improvement in tool scheduling. Code and data are available at https://github.com/MICLAB-BUPT/VTO.

</details>


### [82] [Ego-OSCAR: Egocentric Open source Stereo CAptuRe System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08285)
*Gunjan Paul,Senthil Palanisamy,Satpal Singh Rathore,Pratyush Kumar Patnaik,Shubhanshu Khatana,Abhishek Anand*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Ego-OSCAR，一个开源硬件、低成本（每台不到200美元）的头戴式双目惯性数据采集设备，配套完整软件栈和约550小时带同步IMU的自我中心视角视频数据集，旨在降低大规模众包自我中心数据采集的技术门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究级自我中心采集设备（如Project Aria）成本高昂，难以大规模部署和众包采集；本文旨在提供一个低成本、开放式、可复制的方案，降低任何团队大规模采集自我中心数据的门槛。

Method: 采用硬件同步的全局快门双目相机配合6轴IMU，嵌入式Linux SBC进行设备端视频编码，实时微控制器负责用户反馈和看门狗功能；全部使用商用组件和3D打印部件，成本控制在200美元以内；同时发布完整的软件栈（录像流水线、IMU采样服务、时间同步工具、看门狗固件）以及由分布式贡献者在日常室内环境中采集的约550小时标注数据（开放词汇动作标注、逐帧3D手部重建、逐会话立体标定）。

Result: 实现了每台成本低于200美元的开源双目惯性采集设备，成功收集了约550小时带同步IMU的自我中心双目视频，并提供了完整的开放标注数据集和软硬件设计。

Conclusion: Ego-OSCAR不追求与研究级系统（如Project Aria）相当的单元级保真度，而是定位为最经济可行的众包自我中心数据采集基础平台，通过全面开源来降低任何团队大规模采集自我中心数据的激活能量。

Abstract: We present Ego-OSCAR, an open-hardware, low-cost, head-mounted stereo-inertial capture device for egocentric data collection in the wild. EgoOSCAR pairs a hardware-synchronized global-shutter stereo camera with a 6- axis IMU, an embedded Linux SBC for on-device video encoding, and a realtime microcontroller for user feedback and watchdog functions. The complete bill of materials is under USD 200 per unit, using only commercially available components and 3D-printed parts. Alongside the device, we release a complete software stack (hardware-accelerated recording pipeline, IMU sampling daemon, time-synchronization tooling, and watchdog firmware) and roughly 550 hours of egocentric stereo video per camera with synchronized IMU, collected by a distributed contributor network across everyday indoor environments. The release is annotated rather than raw: free-form action captions cover essentially the entire recorded timeline with an open vocabulary, and per-frame 3D hand reconstructions ship alongside per-session stereo calibration. Ego-OSCAR does not aim to match the per-unit fidelity of research-grade systems such as Project Aria; it aims to be the cheapest defensible substrate for crowdsourced egocentric capture, and to lower the activation energy for any team that wants to collect egocentric data at scale. All hardware designs, software, and the dataset are open-sourced

</details>


### [83] [What Irregularity Costs: CUDA C++, Rust, and Triton on a Hash-Blocked GPU Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08287)
*Petr Korolev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在GPU上实现了相同的哈希分块TSDF融合核函数，分别用CUDA C++、Rust（通过cuda-oxide）和Triton编写，并在不规则的开放寻址哈希表工作负载上进行比较。结果表明：在规则阶段三种语言性能接近；在不规则阶段Rust接近手写CUDA，而Triton慢了一个数量级以上。性能差距源于语言无法表达特定操作，而非代码质量。Triton的编译时有界探测循环还带来正确性问题，会静默丢弃数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GPU语言对比几乎都基于平铺稠密线性代数（每个工具链都表现良好），无法揭示不同语言在不规则、数据依赖型工作负载上的真实差距。作者希望通过一个与常规基准完全相反的负载——开放寻址哈希表（含竞争性写入、每通道数据依赖探测深度）来评估不同GPU编程语言的实际表达能力。

Method: 在三种语言中实现相同的哈希分块TSDF融合核函数：CUDA C++（手写）、Rust（通过NVIDIA的cuda-oxide库）和Triton。工作负载包含规则阶段（遍历截断波段并累加）和不规则阶段（探测并插入开放寻址哈希表），使用硬件计数器分析性能差异来源。

Result: 规则阶段三种语言性能相差不大；不规则阶段Rust接近手写CUDA C++，而Triton慢了一个数量级以上。Triton的代价源于必须运行到编译时边界的探测循环和tl.atomic_cas不支持掩码导致需要额外scratch结构。Rust虽然指令数更少、寄存器更少、占用率相同，却因GPU作用域原子加载的缓存一致性要求绕过了L1缓存而变慢。此外发现Triton的有界探测在常规负载因子下会静默丢弃块导致重建丢失表面补丁。

Conclusion: 语言选择在通常被基准测试的工作负载上几乎免费，但在未被测试的不规则工作负载上代价高昂。差异不是代码质量比率的体现，而是语言表达能力局限所致。作者还修复了cuda-oxide中一个已合并上游的缺陷（作用域原子加载/存储在真实内核构建模式下无法调用）。

Abstract: GPU language comparisons are almost always run on tiled dense linear algebra, where every toolchain is good and the differences are small. We implement the same hash-blocked TSDF fusion kernel in CUDA C++, in Rust through NVIDIA's cuda-oxide, and in Triton, and measure it on a workload with the opposite character: an open-addressed hash table with compare-exchange insertion, data-dependent per-lane probe depth, and contended scatter.
  The result is a split. On the regular stage, which walks a truncation band and accumulates, all three languages land within a small factor of each other. On the irregular stage, which probes and inserts, Rust stays close to hand-written CUDA C++ while Triton is more than an order of magnitude slower. Language choice is nearly free on the work that is usually benchmarked and expensive on the work that is not.
  We attribute both gaps to specific things the languages cannot express, not to ratios. Triton's cost follows from a probe loop that must run to a compile-time bound and from tl.atomic_cas taking no mask, which forces a scratch structure with no counterpart in CUDA. Rust's cost was invisible in every instruction count: its kernel issues fewer instructions, fewer compare-exchanges and fewer registers at identical occupancy, yet was slower. Hardware counters located it in L1 residency. A GPU-scope atomic load must be coherent across SMs, no NVIDIA L1 is, so the type-correct way to read a shared location bypasses the cache on every access.
  Triton's bounded probe is also a correctness problem for fusion: at load factors an ordinary depth trajectory reaches, it silently discards blocks and the reconstruction loses patches of surface with nothing reported. We also report a defect found and fixed in cuda-oxide itself, now merged upstream: its scoped atomic load and store could not be called at all in the build mode that produces real kernels.

</details>


### [84] [Test-Time Prototype Adaptation for Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08290)
*Haozhe Wang,Jintao Cheng,Weibin Li,Xiaoyu Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出测试时原型自适应（TPA），一种无需训练的即插即用模块，在输出层操作而不修改宿主模型的内部计算和权重，通过轻量级转导自适应阶段构建DINO特征原型库，并利用余弦相似度辅助评分线性融合进宿主模型的logits中，显著提升开放词汇语义分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇语义分割（OVSS）方法要么重新设计CLIP的内部注意力，要么注入辅助视觉基础模型的特征，这些方法都需要访问宿主的内部计算并针对其特定前向传播定制。作者希望提出一种在输出层面操作的通用即插即用方案，无需修改宿主内部结构和权重，即可提升分割性能。

Method: TPA包含两个阶段：1）转导自适应阶段，在少量无标注部署域图像上，利用宿主模型自身的输出预测识别置信锚点块，并将其冻结的DINO特征聚合成每类原型；2）推理阶段，通过余弦相似度查找冻结原型库获得辅助评分，与宿主logits线性融合。整个过程无需训练、无需修改宿主前向传播和权重。

Result: TPA在五种代表性OVSS宿主（涵盖注意力重设计和VFM注入两类设计）、三种CLIP主干、八个基准及多种内部VFM选择上，使用单一超参数集且无需逐宿主调参或参数更新，均能一致提升分割精度；仅需约10%的无标注部署域图像即可在多数基准上有效构建原型库。

Conclusion: TPA验证了在输出层进行测试时自适应的可行性，表明无需侵入宿主内部即可有效增强CLIP的密集预测能力，为OVSS提供了一种通用的、低成本的后处理增强范式。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) repurposes a pretrained CLIP encoder for dense prediction without additional labeled supervision. Existing methods improve CLIP's spatial behavior either by redesigning its internal attention or by injecting features from auxiliary vision foundation models; both require access to the host's internal computation and are tailored to its specific forward pass. In this work, we propose Test-time Prototype Adaptation (TPA), a training-free plug-in that operates at the output level, leaving the host's forward pass and weights unmodified. By leveraging a lightweight transductive adaptation phase, TPA identifies confident anchor patches from the host's own output predictions on a small pool of unlabeled deployment-domain images, and aggregates their frozen DINO features into per-class prototypes; at inference, a single cosine similarity lookup against this frozen bank provides an auxiliary score fused linearly with the host's logits. TPA composes with five representative OVSS hosts spanning attention-redesign and VFM-injection designs, across three CLIP backbones, eight benchmarks, and multiple internal VFM choices. Under a single set of hyper-parameters and without per-host tuning or parameter updates, TPA consistently improves segmentation accuracy, with as few as approximately 10% of unlabeled deployment-domain images sufficing for effective bank construction on most benchmarks.

</details>


### [85] [Frequency-Domain Dual-Branch Fusion for Medical Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08307)
*Yusra Tariq,Rakesh Chandra Joshi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双分支频域融合模块用于医学视觉问答，通过基于问题的频谱滤波自适应选择低频结构和高频细节，结合冻结的BiomedCLIP编码器和BioBART解码器，在PMC-VQA上预训练并在VQA-RAD和SLAKE上微调，验证了频域感知融合的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VQA的多模态融合方法在空间域操作，未能充分挖掘视觉和文本表征中互补的频域信息（如图像中的病灶纹理、边界锐度、弥散密度变化等细微视觉证据），需要一种能够利用频域信息进行自适应融合的方法。

Method: 提出双分支频域融合模块，根据输入问题对频谱进行条件滤波，自适应选择全局低频结构和细粒度高频细节后重构空间表征。从冻结的BiomedCLIP编码器早期纹理敏感层和最终语义层提取互补特征，使用对称InfoNCE目标将两者与问题表征对齐，然后与BioBART解码器进行分阶段联合训练。

Result: 在PMC-VQA上预训练并在VQA-RAD和SLAKE基准上微调，实验表明频域感知的多模态融合提高了医学VQA性能，同时保持了轻量高效的架构。

Conclusion: 频域信息感知的多模态融合能够有效提升医学VQA的性能，证明问题条件化的频谱滤波机制有助于更精准地捕捉与临床问题相关的视觉证据。

Abstract: Medical Visual Question Answering (VQA) requires aligning subtle visual evidence, including lesion texture, boundary sharpness, and diffuse density changes, with clinical language. Existing multimodal fusion approaches operating in the spatial domain may not fully exploit complementary frequency information present in visual and textual representations. We introduce a dual-branch frequency-domain fusion module that conditions spectral filtering on the input question, enabling adaptive selection of global low-frequency structure and fine-grained high-frequency detail before reconstructing the spatial representation for answer generation. To provide a richer spectrum for filtering, we extract complementary features from early texture-sensitive and final semantic layers of a frozen BiomedCLIP encoder and align both with the question representation using a symmetric InfoNCE objective prior to staged joint training with a BioBART decoder. We pretrain the proposed model on PMC-VQA and fine-tune it on the VQA-RAD and SLAKE benchmarks, demonstrating that frequency-aware multimodal fusion improves medical VQA performance while maintaining a lightweight and efficient architecture.

</details>


### [86] [Open-World Semantic Segmentation with Sensitivity Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08308)
*Anastasios Romanos Varvarigos,Nikos Giakoumoglou,Tania Stathaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三解码器统一编码器架构，用于开放世界语义分割任务。在双解码器基线（高斯原型闭集分割+对比学习未知区域检测）基础上，新增一个灵敏度解码器，通过捕捉细粒度纹理异常和激活不稳定性来检测语义不确定性，三个解码器提供互补信号（logit空间的OOD距离、嵌入空间的全局特征能量、编码器尺度的局部激活不稳定性）。在Cityscapes和BDD-Anomaly数据集上取得优于基线的异常分割和新类发现性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉系统需在开放世界场景中运行，既要识别已知类别，又要检测未见或异常内容。传统语义分割模型基于"封闭世界"假设，对新颖内容常产生过度自信的错误分类，因此需要无需额外监督就能同时分割已知类并检测/分组新类的方法。

Method: 在统一编码器-解码器设计中，将双解码器基线扩展为三解码器架构：(1) 第一解码器使用高斯原型进行已知类别的闭集分割；(2) 第二解码器通过对比特征学习在嵌入空间中隔离未知区域；(3) 第三解码器（核心贡献）为灵敏度解码器，捕捉细粒度纹理不规则性和激活不稳定性，反映语义不确定性。三类信号互补：logit空间的类级OOD距离、嵌入空间的全局特征能量、跨编码器尺度的局部激活不稳定性。

Result: 在Cityscapes和BDD-Anomaly数据集上，该方法改善了异常分割和新类发现性能，同时保持具有竞争力的闭集精度。在BDD-Anomaly上相比基线，AUROC提升+2.4%，FPR@95TPR降低2.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 三个解码器提供真正互补的信号，能够更全面地检测开放世界中的未知内容。新增的灵敏度解码器弥补了语义原型和对比范数无法可靠检测的局部纹理异常和激活不稳定问题，验证了多解码器互补设计在开放世界语义分割中的有效性。

Abstract: Modern vision systems must operate in "open-world" settings, where models must recognize known categories and detect unseen or anomalous content. Conventional semantic segmentation models operate under a "closed-world" assumption, often producing overconfident misclassifications on novel content. We address open-world semantic segmentation, the joint task of segmenting known classes while detecting and grouping novel or anomalous content without additional supervision, by extending a dual-decoder baseline with a third, complementary decoder within a unified encoder-decoder design. The first decoder performs closed-set segmentation using Gaussian prototypes for known categories. The second uses contrastive feature learning to isolate unknown regions in embedding space. The third, our key contribution, is a sensitivity decoder that captures fine-grained texture irregularities and activation instabilities indicative of semantic uncertainty, which neither semantic prototypes nor contrastive norms can reliably detect. The three decoders provide genuinely complementary signals: class-level OOD distance in logit space, global feature energy in embedding space, and local activation instability across encoder scales. Experiments on Cityscapes and BDD-Anomaly show that our method improves anomaly segmentation and novel-class discovery while maintaining competitive closed-set accuracy, with gains of +2.4% AUROC and a 2.5 pp. reduction in FPR@95TPR on BDD-Anomaly over the baseline.

</details>


### [87] [Three Necessary Principles for Self-Supervised Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08309)
*Nikos Giakoumoglou,Paschalis Giakoumoglou,Tania Stathaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出无标签视觉表征学习需要三个互不重叠的训练目标共同作用：跨视图语义不变性（观测）、块级空间预测（预测）和表征非退化（正则化），并通过理论证明与对照实验验证三者缺一不可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法往往只关注单一训练信号（如对比学习或预测任务），缺乏对训练目标完整性的系统理论分析。作者希望回答：无标签视觉表征学习究竟需要哪些不可替代的训练信号，以及各主流方法为何有效或失效。

Method: 将训练目标形式化为观测、预测、正则化三大原则；通过数学证明分析不同目标组合的最小化行为（包括常数编码器陷阱、梯度互补性、动量编码器收敛性）；将对比对齐的崩溃抵抗力形式化为梯度衰减论证；将各类主流自监督方法统一到一个能量分解框架下，并用对照实验（含块检索评估）验证每个理论声明。

Result: 证明了观测+预测无正则化时常数编码器是全局最小解；两个目标在编码器输出处梯度互补且结构不冲突；动量编码器收敛于与在线编码器相同的固定点但不提供崩溃保证；对比对齐只提供有限的崩溃抵抗；缺失任何单一目标都无法被另外两个目标替代。

Conclusion: 观测、预测、正则化三大目标构成了无标签视觉表征学习的完整且互不可替代的训练信号，所有主流自监督方法均可视为该统一能量分解的特例。

Abstract: We argue that learning visual representations without labels requires a training signal jointly complete across three non-overlapping objectives: semantic invariance across augmented views, patch-level spatial prediction, and representational non-degeneracy. We formalize these as the observation, prediction, and regularization principles and prove (i) that combining observation and prediction without regularization admits the constant encoder as a global minimizer under negative-free alignment; (ii) that the two objectives are gradient-complementary and structurally non-conflicting at the encoder output; and (iii) that the momentum encoder converges to the same fixed point as the online encoder and provides no collapse guarantee at convergence. Contrastive alignment provides only self-limiting collapse resistance, formalized via an explicit gradient-decay argument. Dropping prediction withholds the spatial training signal by construction; dropping observation forfeits cross-view semantic invariance by construction; at the scale we study, no pair substitutes for the third. Every major self-supervised method is a special case of a single unified energy decomposition. We pair every theoretical claim with a controlled experiment, including a patch-retrieval evaluation for the spatial consequence of prediction.

</details>


### [88] [Your VLM Already Knows When: Training-Free Temporal Grounding by Asking Yes or No](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08315)
*Ji Huang,Barry Devereux,Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 多模态大语言模型在时间定位任务中表现极差（如Charades-STA上R@0.5仅3.8%），且错误预测大多伴随低输出熵（自信地错误）。研究发现问题根源在于任务接口而非感知能力。通过将时间戳回归替换为二分问题的由粗到细扫描（仅用首词概率做排序），在冻结骨干网络上将R@0.5提升28-50个点。残差错误分解为感知轴（随骨干变化）和几何轴（可解析预测）。提出的免训练方法FV-Action在Charades-STA上达到56.8% R@0.5，超越同期最强免训练结果及多个监督方法的零样本表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在事件识别上可靠，但时间定位能力极弱，且传统的熵基错误检测方法完全失效（低于随机分类器）。作者旨在揭示并解决这一"自信地错误"的时间定位失败问题，无需任何训练即可提升性能。

Method: （1）任务接口重构：放弃时间戳回归，改为由粗到细的二分问答扫描，仅对首词概率做排序而非直接取概率值；（2）残差错误分解：提出感知轴（源于骨干网络的事件感知局限）和几何轴（由输出窗口与事件宽度之比决定的解析可预测因素）两个可度量维度；（3）提出FV-Action：基于上述分析的免训练方法，无需任何时间监督。

Result: 在四个冻结骨干网络上R@0.5提升28-50个百分点；FV-Action在Charades-STA上达到56.8% R@0.5，超过同骨干的本地grounding管线及最强免训练结果；在TACoS上超越所有TVG-trained模型的零样本表现；在ActivityNet Captions和QVHighlights上优于直接预测。

Conclusion: 多模态大语言模型时间定位失败的根本原因在于任务接口设计而非感知能力。采用二分问答的粗到细扫描可以大幅解锁冻结模型的时序理解潜力，而将残差错误分解为感知与几何两个可解释轴，为免训练的视频时间定位提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs that recognise events reliably still fail to say when they happen. Prompted for timestamps, strong VLMs reach as little as $3.8\%$ R@0.5 on Charades-STA, and $77$ to $80\%$ of their wrong predictions carry low output entropy: the models are confidently wrong, and entropy-based error detection stays below a random classifier. We show that this failure lives in the task interface, not in perception. Holding the weights fixed, replacing timestamp regression with a coarse-to-fine scan of binary questions, whose first-token probabilities are consumed only as a ranking, raises R@0.5 by $28$ to $50$ points across four frozen backbones. The residual failures decompose into two measurable axes: a perception axis that moves with the backbone, and a geometry axis that is analytically predictable from the ratio of the output-window and event widths. FV-Action, the training-free method built on this analysis, reaches $56.8\%$ R@0.5 on Charades-STA, above the same backbone's native grounding pipeline and the strongest training-free result on this benchmark; it surpasses every TVG-trained model evaluated zero-shot on TACoS, and improves over direct prediction on ActivityNet Captions and QVHighlights, with no temporal supervision at any stage.

</details>


### [89] [A continually expandable foundation model for brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08319)
*Michail Mamalakis,Carmen Jimenez-Mesa,Yonghao Li,Hao Chen,Chao Li,Antonios Mamalakis,John Suckling,Richard Bethlehem,Stephen J. Price,Richard J. Gilbertson,Pietro Lio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Alcmaeon，一个基于超过42.5万个容积的三维脑MRI基础模型，通过Graph-Blueprint Pruning (GBP)技术实现跨临床领域的顺序扩展，在防止遗忘的同时保留可训练容量，并支持多种下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑MRI模型通常针对单一疾病、人群或成像协议开发；基础模型虽承诺通用表示，但预训练后在更新新数据时容易丢失早期能力，缺乏可持续增长且不遗忘的范式。

Method: Alcmaeon结合体积编码与潜扩散生成，采用Graph-Blueprint Pruning (GBP)策略：保护对早期域重要的网络模块（蓝图），同时保持其余容量可训练。在健康老化、神经退行、发育、精神及肿瘤影像等多个临床域上进行顺序扩展。

Result: 在体素级重建指标上，GBP比顺序适应和弹性权重巩固（EWC）表现出更少的遗忘，在肿瘤影像适应后优势最大。蓝图提供了模型容量保护和重用过程的可检查记录。不同模型层的表示分别支持图像合成、疾病分类、生存建模和术后预测，但单一表示无法对所有任务最优。

Conclusion: Alcmaeon展示了脑MRI基础模型可以随新数据增长同时保留早期能力的可行路径，为可持续扩展的医学影像基础模型提供了方向。

Abstract: Brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is central to neuroscience and clinical assessment, but models are commonly developed for individual diseases, populations or imaging protocols. Foundation models promise more general representations, yet they are usually pretrained once and can lose earlier capabilities when updated with new data. Here we show that Alcmaeon, a three-dimensional brain MRI foundation model pretrained without manual labels on more than 425,000 volumes and derived imaging maps, can be expanded sequentially across clinical domains. Alcmaeon combines volumetric encoding and latent diffusion generation with Graph-Blueprint Pruning (GBP), which protects network modules important to earlier domains while leaving the remaining capacity trainable. Across expansion from healthy ageing and neurodegeneration to developmental, psychiatric and tumour imaging, GBP showed less forgetting than sequential adaptation and elastic weight consolidation across voxel-level reconstruction measures, with its largest advantage after adaptation to tumour imaging. The blueprints provided an inspectable record of how model capacity was protected and reused. Representations from different model levels supported image synthesis, disease classification, survival modelling and postoperative prediction, although no single representation was optimal for every task. These findings provide a route towards brain MRI foundation models that can grow with emerging data while retaining earlier capabilities.

</details>


### [90] [Circuit Fine-Tuning for Compute-Efficient Transformer Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08336)
*Uri Z. Kialy,Gil Ben-Artzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Circuit Fine-Tuning (CFT)，一种计算高效的参数高效微调框架，通过电路发现（circuit discovery）在训练前选择需要微调的模块。与传统的归因方法不同，CFT使用近零初始化的探针头而非训练好的任务头进行归因，从而隔离骨干网络对目标分布的响应。CFT仅需微调恢复出的子图，无需学习率预热，平均约20个epoch即可达到峰值精度（而强PEFT基线需要44-96个epoch），训练FLOPs减少2.3-6.6倍，墙钟时间减少最多16倍，且零参数增加和零推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PEFT方法以参数数量作为主要效率指标，但参数稀疏并不等同于计算高效：参数稀疏的方法仍可能产生全模型训练成本，且通常需要长训练计划才能达到峰值精度。作者希望提出一种真正关注计算效率（FLOPs和墙钟时间）的微调框架。

Method: CFT利用电路发现技术，在训练前选择需要微调的模块子图。关键创新在于将归因分析的目标从传统训练好的任务头改为近零初始化的探针头，这样能隔离骨干网络对目标数据分布的响应，而非特定分类器的偏好。之后仅对恢复出的子图进行微调。

Result: 在VTAB-1k、Swin层级骨干、CBIS-DDSM医学影像及Gemma-3视觉语言模型（CUB-200）等实验上验证了有效性。CFT无需学习率预热，平均约20个epoch达到峰值精度，相比强PEFT基线（44-96个epoch），训练FLOPs减少2.3-6.6倍，墙钟时间最多减少16倍，且添加零参数、零推理操作。

Conclusion: CFT证明计算效率（而非仅参数效率）应成为PEFT的核心优化目标，电路发现作为一种训练前模块选择工具，能够在不牺牲精度的情况下显著降低训练成本，为高效微调提供新的范式。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) has become the de facto standard for adapting Vision Transformers (ViTs) to downstream tasks. While parameter count has been the dominant efficiency metric in PEFT, it does not imply \textit{compute efficiency}: parameter-sparse methods can still incur full-model training cost per step, and typically need long schedules to reach peak accuracy. We introduce Circuit Fine-Tuning (CFT), a compute-efficient framework that uses circuit discovery---conventionally used to explain trained models---to select modules for fine-tuning before training. Whereas attribution is conventionally formulated against a trained task head, we formulate it against a near-zero-initialized probe head, which isolates the response of the backbone to the target distribution rather than the preferences of a particular classifier. CFT then fine-tunes only the recovered subgraph. CFT needs no learning-rate warmup and reaches peak accuracy in ${\sim}20$ epochs on average---versus $44$--$96$ for strong PEFT baselines---yielding $2.3$--$6.6\times$ fewer training FLOPs and up to $16\times$ less wall-clock time, while adding zero parameters and no inference operations. Experiments across a standard visual transfer benchmark (VTAB-1k), hierarchical backbones (Swin), domain-shifted medical imaging (CBIS-DDSM), and a vision-language model (Gemma-3 on CUB-200) demonstrate the effectiveness of CFT. Code is available at https://github.com/UriKialy/CFT

</details>


### [91] [Tropical Cyclone Forecasting via Latent Rectified Flow using Satellite Imagery and Atmospheric Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08354)
*Meheru Zannat,Sk. Md. Masudul Ahsan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个单次前向传播的深度生成模型，同时预测热带气旋的卫星红外图像和四个ERA5大气场，输出至未来9小时。模型采用五通道变分自编码器压缩时空帧，结合条件整流流UNet和因子化时间注意力模块，并通过DRaFT奖励微调（基于转向流计算的微分轨迹误差）优化路径预测。在2022年留出风暴数据上，PSNR达16.35 dB、SSIM为0.759，采样速度比级联扩散基线快约30倍，+9h轨迹误差62.4 km，较基线降低15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带气旋在气候变化下破坏性增强，需要高效预测。现有深度生成模型要么只能生成卫星图像要么只能生成大气场，无法同时输出两者；且需要大量采样步骤，硬件要求高；风暴轨迹来自回归头，与生成的大气物理过程缺乏直接联系。

Method: 构建单次前向传播的统一生成模型：1）五通道VAE将5×256×256帧压缩至4×64×64隐空间；2）条件整流流UNet配合因子化时间注意力模块，从过去3帧预测未来3帧（输入含最佳轨迹坐标和时间戳）；3）通过DRaFT奖励微调，利用从预测风场计算的转向流推导可微轨迹误差进行优化。

Result: 在2022年留出风暴上：PSNR 16.35 dB，SSIM 0.759，所有提前时间均优于复现的级联扩散基线（+9h时+0.84 dB）；采样速度快约30倍（56 ms vs 1673 ms）；+9h轨迹误差62.4 km，较基线降低15%；奖励微调进一步在不同采样预算下将轨迹误差降低8-11%。

Conclusion: 该单次生成模型证明了同时预测卫星图像与大气场、并借助物理耦合的轨迹误差优化，能够在显著降低计算成本的同时提升预测精度，为热带气旋预报提供了一种高效且物理一致的替代方案。

Abstract: Tropical cyclones are growing more destructive in a changing climate, and efficient forecasting of their structure and track has become a necessity. Deep generative models promise an alternative to computationally expensive numerical weather prediction (NWP), yet current systems produce either satellite imagery or atmospheric fields, never both; they need many sampling steps, putting them out of reach of modest hardware; and their storm tracks come from regression heads with no physical link to the generated atmosphere. This work presents a single-pass model that jointly forecasts GRIDSAT-B1 infrared imagery and four ERA5 atmospheric fields (U-wind, V-wind, air temperature, and surface pressure) out to nine hours. A five-channel variational autoencoder compresses each 5 x 256 x 256 frame to a 4 x 64 x 64 latent, and a conditional rectified-flow UNet with a factorized temporal-attention module predicts the next three frames from three past frames, their best-track coordinates, and timestamps. The model is then reward-fine-tuned (DRaFT) against a differentiable track error derived from the predicted winds through a steering-flow calculation. On held-out 2022 storms the model reaches 16.35 dB PSNR and 0.759 SSIM, ahead of a reproduced cascaded-diffusion baseline at every lead time (+0.84 dB at +9 h) while sampling ~30x faster (56 ms vs. 1673 ms). Track error at +9 h is 62.4 km, 15% below the baseline, and a reward fine-tuning study demonstrates a further 8-11% track-error reduction across sampler budgets.

</details>


### [92] [VOICE: A Vision-Omics Foundation Model Integrating Direct and Retrieval-Based Prediction of In-situ Single-Cell Gene Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08366)
*Xin Luo,Yicheng Tao,Haoxuan Zeng,Suyuan Wang,Chenzi Ouyang,Meiqi Zhu,Kai Liu,Shuibing Chen,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VOICE是一个多模态基础模型，利用配对的Xenium数据从H&E图像预测单细胞基因表达，通过对比学习对齐形态与表达嵌入，并用双分支（直接回归+参考检索）融合的方式提升预测性能，在七个指标上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学虽然能以单细胞分辨率解析基因表达，但成本高昂、局限于数百到数千个基因的靶向panel，且仅适用于少量样本。相比之下，H&E染色成像廉价且常规大规模采集，因此从形态学预测单细胞表达是将分子分析扩展到大型组织样本库的实用途径。

Method: VOICE首先利用病理基础模型提取以细胞为中心的H&E形态特征，并与转录组基础模型的单细胞表达嵌入通过对比学习进行对齐（基于2300万细胞训练）。随后通过两个分支预测表达：一个分支直接从形态学回归表达；另一个分支从相似参考细胞中检索实测表达，以恢复缺乏形态信号的基因。最后通过逐基因权重融合两个分支的输出，因为不同基因的形态学可预测性各不相同。
